patippurimi aṟṟatu

inta ākkattuṭaṉ toṭarpuṭiyavarkaḷ, ulakaḷaḷāviya potup payaṉpāṭṭukku eṉa patippurimic caṭṭattukku uṭpaṭṭu, taṅkaḷ aṉittup patippurimikaḷiyum viṭuvittuḷḷaṉar.

nīṅkaḷ ivvākkattip paṭiyeṭukkalām; mēmpaṭuttalām; pakiralām; vēṟu vaṭivamāka māṟṟalām; vaṇikap payaṉkaḷum aṭiyalām. ivaṟṟukku nīṅkaḷ opputal ētum kōrat tēviyilli.

piṉṉiṇippukaḷ





1

kiḻakkum mēṟkum





īrāyirattu ainūṟu āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉarē, tamiḻakattiṟkum kirēkka, urōma, pāpilōṉiya, pāracīka, arapu nāṭukaḷukkumiṭiyē nalla vaṇikat toṭarpukaḷ iruntati varalāṟṟuk kuṟippukaḷ viḷampukiṉṟaṉa. namatu nāṭṭiṉ taḷḷāviḷiyuḷi paṇṭamāṟṟilē peṟṟuk koṇṭa mēṉāṭṭu vaṇikarkaḷ, avikaḷukkup pakaramāka taṅkaḷ nāṭṭiṉ putumip poruṭkaḷi tamiḻnāṭṭiṟku vaḻaṅkiṉar. tamiḻakattiṉ akilum tukilum, paruttiyaṇiyum pavaḷamaṇiyum, muttum miḷakum, mēṉāṭukaḷil mikutiyākap payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa.[1] kirēkka nāṭṭu maturacaṅkaḷ, kaṇṇāṭik kalacaṅkaḷ, īyam takaram, ākiyavikaḷum taṅka, veḷḷik kācukaḷum tamiḻarkaḷik kavarntaṉa. ekiptu nāṭṭuk kaṭaṟpaṭṭiṉamāṉa alaksāntiriyāvi miyamākak koṇṭu arapu nāṭukaḷ vaḻiyāka, kīḻnāṭukaḷukkum, pālattiṉam vaḻiyāka kirēkka nāṭṭiṟkum, vaṇikac cāttu vaḻikaḷ ceṉṟaṉa.[2]

ivi varalāṟṟiṉ kāla varampiṟkuṭpaṭāta kālantoṭṭu iruntu vantati viviliyam (pipiḷ) cāṉṟu pakarkiṉṟatu. ki.mu. 1000-l siriyā nāṭṭil mati maṉṉaṉāka viḷaṅkiya sālamaṉ, jerusēlam nakaril amittu irunta pirammāṇṭamāṉa, tēvālayattik kāṇpataṟkuc ceṉṟa ēmaṉ nāṭṭu ṣēpā araciyār, anta maṉṉaṉukku teṉṉāṭṭil ciṟanta vācaṉip poruṭkaḷiyum akiliyum tukiliyum poṉṉuṭaṉ aṉpaḷippāka aḷittati “putiya ēṟpāṭu” vivarittuḷḷatu.[3] kirēkka nūlāciriyarāṉa hekṭariyas milēṭṭas ki mu 549-486 intiya nāṭṭiṉ pala paṭṭaṇaṅkaḷi ciṟappāka mēṟku kiḻakku kaṭal tuṟikaḷi kuṟippiṭṭu iruppatāl, antak kāla kaṭṭattil intiyāviṟkum kirēkkattiṟkum, tamiḻnāṭṭiṟkum iṭiyē nikaḻnta virivāṉa vaṇikattiṉ taṉmiyi ūkikka utavukiṟatu. itaṉip pōṉṟu, rōm uḷḷiṭṭa matyatarikkaṭal nāṭukaḷuṭaṉ tamiḻakam toṭarpu koṇṭu iruntati, karūr, kōvi, maturi, aḻakaṉkuḷam ākiya ūr akaḻvukaḷil kiṭitta rōma nāṇayaṅkaḷ uṟutippaṭuttukiṉṟaṉa. aṅṅaṉamē, namatu nāṭṭup poruṭkaḷum mēṟku nāṭukaḷiṉ āṭampara vāḻkkikkup payaṉpaṭṭati kīḻēkaṇṭa varalāṟṟu nikaḻccikaḷ viḷakkukiṉṟaṉa.

ki.mu. 210-l rōmāpuriyil iyaṟki eytiya cilā eṉṟa maṉṉaṉatu citiyilum nīrō maṉṉaṉatu (ki.mu, 68-54) uṟaviṉarāṉa pāppōyiyiṉ īmaccaṭaṅkiṉ poḻutum, kīḻ nāṭukaḷil iruntu rōmāpurikku varavaḻikkappaṭṭa vācaṉipporuṭkaḷ aṉittum koṭṭappaṭṭu eriyūṭṭappaṭṭatu.[4] inta vācaṉip poruṭkaḷukku aṭuttapaṭiyāka rōma nāṭṭil, tamiḻnāṭṭu muttukkaḷ iṭam piṭittiruntaṉa. pēraracar jūliyas sīsar (ki.mu. 39-14) taṉatu naṇparāṉa puruṭṭasiṉ tāyārukku, 48, 457 pavuṉ peṟumāṉamuḷḷa naṉmuttukkaḷi aṉpaḷippāka vaḻaṅkiya ceytiyum uḷḷatu.[5] pēraḻaki kiḷiyō pātrāviṉ kātaṇikaḷil 1,51,457 pavuṉ peṟumāṉa muttukkaḷ iṭam peṟṟiruntaṉavām.[6] inta muttukkaḷ aṉṟiya tamiḻakattil ciṟappuṟṟirunta paṭṭiṉaṅkaḷil iruntu peṟappaṭṭavi eṉpatu teḷivu. appoḻutu cēranāṭṭil, koṭumaṇam, pantar eṉṟa kaṭaṟṟuṟikaḷ muttukkaḷukku peyar peṟṟiruntami,

“koṭumaṉam paṭṭa viṉimā ṉeṭuṅkalam

pantar payanta palar pukaḻ muttam” ... ... ... eṉṟa





aracil kiḻāratu eṭṭām pattu, iyampukiṟatu. iṉṉum, atē nūliṉ ēḻāmpattilum, oṉpatām pattilum inta ūrkaḷip paṟṟiya pukaḻurikaḷ potintu uḷḷaṉa.[7]

ki.mu. aintāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭil, pāpilōṉil pāracīka, maṉṉaṉ tārīyasatu āṭciyiṉ poḻutu, vāṇikac cāttākac ceṉṟa tamiḻarkaḷ aṅkēyē kuṭiyiruppu amittu nilipeṟṟu iruntaṉar. ekiptu nāṭṭil, ammōṉrā eṉṟa iṭattil uḷḷa ālayattil, tamatu kaṭaṟpayaṇatti veṟṟikaramāka muṭitta teṉṉāṭṭār oruvar mēṟkoṇṭa vaḻipāṭṭu nikaḻcci anta nāṭṭuk kalveṭṭu oṉṟil kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. aleksāntiriyāvilum teṉṉāṭṭār kuṭiyiruppu iruntati aṟiya inta kalveṭṭu utavukiṟatu.[8]

kiṟistu cakāptam tuvaṅkiya piṉṉarum, kiḻakku mēṟku toṭarpukaḷ nīṭittaṉa. alaksāntiriyāvilum, ēṭaṉilum celvam koḻittatu. aṅkē arāpiyak kuṭiyēṟṟaṅkaḷ pala eḻuntaṉa. tamiḻakattil iruntu rōmap pēraracu kiḷāṭiyas cīsar avikku araciyal tūtukkuḻu vantati ki.pi. 41l piḷiṉi kuṟittuḷḷār.[9]

paruvakkāṟṟukaḷiṉ taṉmikaḷi virivāka ārāyntu aṟinta ekiptiya mālumiyāṉa uṟippālas ki pi. 45-l arapiya kaṭal payaṇattil putiya tiruppatti ēṟpaṭuttiṉār.[10] atuvari, rōma, arāpiyak kappalkaḷ, ceṅkaṭal, pāracīkak﻿kuṭākkaṭal, arāpiyak kaṭal vaḻiyāka kaṭaṟkarip pakutikaḷil kariyi oṭṭiyavāṟu intiyāvukku ceṉṟaṉa. ivvitam celvatil kappalkaḷ pāṟikaḷil mōtutal, kaṭaṟkoḷḷikku ilakkākutal pōṉṟa āpattukkaḷ malintu iruntaṉa. āṉāl uṟippālaḷiṉ putiya vaḻi, āḻkaṭalip paruva kālaṅkaḷil nērākak kaṭantu cellu muṟiyākum. arāpiya tīpakaṟpa muṉiyāṉa siyākaras eṉṟa iṭattiliruntu tamiḻakattiṉ mēṟkuk kariyi aṭiya appoḻutu nāṟpatu nāṭkaḷē āyiṉa. payaṇam ceyya vēṇṭiya motta tuāram 1355 milkaḷākak kuṟintatu. kālamum tolivum kuṟintatuṭaṉ, vāṇipak kuḻuviṉarukku kariyōrap pakutikaḷil uḷḷa kaṭaṟkoḷḷiyariṉiṉṟum viṭutaliyum kiṭṭiyatu. muṉṉar, intiyāviṉ mēṟku karikku āṇṭu oṉṟiṟku, arēpiyāvil iruntu irupatu kappalkaḷē ceṉṟu vantaṉa. āṉāl ippoḻutu arāpiya tīpakaṟpattiliruntu nāḷoṉṟiṟku oru kappal vītam kiḻakku nōkki payaṇam toṭaṅkiyatu.[11] jūṉ jūli mātam mēṟku nōkkit toṭaṅkum kaṭaṟpayaṇam, teṉkiḻakku paruvak kāṟṟu eḻukiṉṟa ṭicampar - jaṉavari mātaṅkaḷil, muṭivu peṟṟu kappalkaḷ tāyakam tirumpiṉa.

ki.pi. mutal nūṟṟāṇṭil, kīḻtticiyil payaṇam ceyta “peripulūs” nūliṉ āciriyaratu kuṟippukaḷum, ki pi. iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭil payaṇam ceyta tālamiyiṉ payaṇak kuṟippukaḷum inta vivaraṅkaḷit tarukiṉṟaṉa. urōmarkaḷatu tamiḻ nāṭṭuṭaṉāṉa vāṇipam, appoḻutu mikavum viṟuviṟuppāka naṭipeṟṟatu. avarkaḷ iṅku koṇarntu viṟṟa cāmāṉkaḷiviṭa, iṅkiruntu vāṅkic ceṉṟa cāmāṉkaḷiṉ peṟumāṉam, paṇṭamāṟṟu matippil kūṭutalāka iruntatu. ataṟku īṭāka, avarkaḷ nāṭṭup poṟkācukaḷik koṭuttaṉar iṅṅaṉam kīḻnāṭṭup putumip poruṭkaḷi mikutiyāka vāṅkik koṇṭu ceṉṟataṉ kāraṇamāka rōmanāṭṭu karuvūlam vaṟṟi vaṟaṇṭuviṭṭatāka peṭrōṉiyas eṉṟa rōma nāṭṭu āciriyar kuṟittuḷḷār.[12] tamiḻakatti urōmarkaḷ tāmirikā eṉa vaḻaṅkiṉar. tamiḻakattiṉ mukkiya kaṭaṟtuṟikaḷāṉa muciṟi, kumari, koṟki, toṇṭi, pukār, putuki, ākiya paṭṭiṉaṅkaḷiyum kōvi, uṟiyūr, maturi, aḻakaṉ kuḷam ākiya paṭṭaṇaṅkaḷiyum avar naṉku aṟintu iruntaṉar. intap perunakarkaḷil avarkaḷatu kuṭiyiruppukaḷum niṟuvappaṭṭu iruntaṉa. inta nakaraṅkaḷil kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭa ērāḷamāṉa urōma nāṇayaṅkaḷum, aṇmiyil arikkāmēṭṭil akaḻntu eṭukkappaṭṭa ariya paṇṭaṅkaḷum itaṉi uṟutippaṭuttukiṉṟaṉa.

namatu paḻantamiḻ nūṟkaḷāṉa caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷ urōmarkaḷi yavaṉar, eṉakkuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉa. avarkaḷatu nāṭum yavaṉam eṉṟa potuccolliṉāl vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu. aimperuṅkāppiyaṅkaḷil oṉṟāṉa cilappatikāram-vāḻttuk kāti “vaṉcol yavaṉar vaḷanāṭu” eṉak kuṟippiṭukiṟatu.[13] tamiḻ, vaḻaṅkum patiṉēḻu nilaṅkaḷil, yavaṉamum oṉṟu eṉa aṉṟu karutappaṭṭu vantatu. viṣṇupurāṇam, māḷavi kākkiṉi mittiram ākiya smaskiruta ilakkiyaṅkaḷ, yavaṉam, intiya nāṭṭiṉ mēṟku elliyil amarntiruntatākavum, yavaṉarkaḷ antap pirāntiyattil irunta pūrvakuṭikaḷ eṉavum kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ēṉiya vaṭamoḻi ilakkiyaṅkaḷum, yavaṉarkaḷi, āriyarallāta iṉattiṉarāka, intu camayattiṉiṉṟum māṟupaṭṭavarkaḷāka, anniyarkaḷāka, pāracīkarkaḷāka, cakararkaḷāka, kirēkkarkaḷāka, varṇittuḷḷaṉa. uṟiprū collāṉa “yōṉā” “yōṉ” ākiyavikaḷiṉ oṉṟiṉ aṭippaṭiyil iruntu yavaṉa eṉṟa col tamiḻ vaḻakkiṟku vantuḷḷatu.[14] mēlum, pirākirutac collāṉa lavaṉa (uppu eṉṟa poruḷ) eṉṟa mūlattiṉiṉṟum māṟupaṭṭu intac col uruvāki irukkalām eṉpatu cila moḻi vallunar muṭivu.

ki.mu.250-240aic cērnta acōkaṉatu putta pōtaṉikaḷit tāṅkiya tūṇkaḷil “yōṉā” eṉṟa pālimoḻic ﻿col payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭuḷḷatu. āṉāl, māvīraṉ alaksāntaratu cintu nati paṭiyeṭuppiṟkup piṟaku, intac col intiya varalāṟṟu nūlkaḷil kirēkkarkaḷi maṭṭum kuṟiptuṭaṉ, avarkaḷatu kuṭiyiruppukaḷ yamuṉi āṟṟiṟku mēṟkēyum kujarāt mānilattiṟku teṟkēyum nilittu iruntaṉa eṉpatiyum cuṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. kujarāt mānilattil uḷḷa “jūṉākāt” eṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭum kaṭaṟkarip paṭṭiṉam, yavaṉarkaḷatu iruppiṭam eṉṟa poruḷil vaḻaṅkappaṭṭa “yōṉākar” eṉṟa colliṉ tiripu eṉpatu iṅku kuṟippiṭattakkatu. kāraṇam acōkaṉatu āṭciyil tusaspā eṉṟa yavaṉar. intap pakutiyil āḷunarākap paṇiyāṟṟi iruntatum yavaṉarkaḷ iṅku mikunta eṇṇikkiyil vāḻntu vantatum ākum. iṅkaṉam, intiya nāṭṭila nilittuviṭṭa yavaṉarkaḷ 'nam yavaṉa” eṉa appoḻutu vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatāka karlā kalveṭṭukkaḷ kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa.[15]

itē kālattiya kaṭiccaṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷil, tamiḻakam pōṉṟa yavaṉarkaḷip paṟṟiya kuṟippukaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. “valipuṇar yākki vaṉkaṇ yavaṉar” eṉa mullippāṭṭum,[16] avarkaḷatu “naṉkalam tanta taṇkamaḻ tērali” puṟappāṭṭum,[17] avarkaḷatu “pāvi viḷakku”[18] “otima viḷakku”[19] “makara viṉi” ākiyavikaḷi peruṅkatiyum, “poṉṉoṭu vantu kaṟiyoṭu peyarnta” avarkaḷatu “viṉimāṉ naṉkalatti” akappāṭṭu (eṇ. 140)m kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉa. mēlum, avarkaḷ “kaṭimatil vāyiṟ kāvaliṟ ciṟantu “iruntati” cilappatikāramum “taṇṭamiḻ viṉiñar tammuṭaṉ kūṭippaṇipurinta “yavaṉat taccari” “maṇimēkaliyum” cuṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. ki.pi. mūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ tuvakkattil alaksāntiriyā paṭṭiṉattil nikaḻnta paṭukoliyoṉṟiṉ kāraṇamāka, aṅku kiḻakku mēṟku nāṭukaḷiṉ vaṇika makkaḷ naṭamāṭṭam kuṟintatu. attuṭaṉ, āṭamparattiṉ atīta elliyil iyaṅkik koṇṭirunta rōmap pēraracu ki.pi. 217 l vīḻcci peṟat toṭaṅkiyatu. atuvari kirēkka, rōmarkaḷukku utaviyāḷarkaḷāka irunta arapikaḷ, kīḻi nāṭṭu vaṇikatti tāṅkaḷē mēṟkoṇṭu ciṟappāka naṭatti vantaṉar. kāraṇam arapikaḷukku vācaṉip poruṭkaḷiṉ mītu iyalpāṉa īṭupāṭu iruntu vantatu ākum. ātalāl avarkaḷatu vāṇikattil, puṉuku pōṉṟa intiya nāṭṭu vācaṉip poruṭkaḷum intiya nāṭṭu eḵkiṉāl tayārikkappaṭṭa pōrvāḷkaḷum perum iṭam peṟṟu iruntaṉa.[20] ātalāl islām tōṉṟiya ēḻāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭu mutal yavaṉar, eṉṟa tamiḻc col tamiḻaka islāmiyarik kuṟikka tamiḻil eḻunta orē col eṉa uṟutiyākiviṭṭatu.

iyaṟkiyāka amintuḷḷa uṭalvāku, ūṭṭam tarum uṇavup paḻakkaṅkaḷ, uṟutiyāṉa uḷḷam, ākiya nalliyalpukaḷ kāraṇamāka arapikaḷ, kaṭal toḻilic ciṟappāka mēṟkoṇṭu iruntaṉar. avarkaḷ tokuttu vittirunta puviyiyal ceytikaḷum, palvēṟu nāṭukaḷiṉ kaṭal varipaṭaṅkaḷum, toṉmiyāṉa nūṟkaḷum, avarkaḷatu tirikaṭal ōṭum tiṟamikku takka vaḻikāṭṭikaḷāka amintaṉa. kuṟippāka, intiyāviṟku kaṭal vaḻi kāṇappuṟappaṭṭu, amerikka nilapparappi kaṇṭupiṭitta pōrccukkal mālumiyāṉa kolampasiṉ ārvattiṟku, muṉṉōṭiyāka iruntavi, arapikaḷatu puviiyal koḷkikaḷum, kaṭal vaḻi campantamāṉa varipaṭaṅkaḷumākum. atē ārvattiṉāl untappaṭṭu, āppirikka peruṅkaṇṭatti cuṟṟi ki.pi. 1498-l intiyāviṉ mēṟkuk kariyi aṭinta pōrccukkīsiya nāṭṭu vāskōṭakāmāviṟku kaṭalvaḻi kāṭṭi vantavar ahamatu ipṉumajītu eṉṟa arapiyar ivar kiḻakku āppirikkāviṉ malinti nāṭṭavar.[21] avariṭam kaṭalvaḻi, kāṭṭum varipaṭaṅkaḷum, kaṭaṟ payaṇattiṟkāṉa ciṟanta karuvikaḷum iruntaṉa.

ātalāl, peruṅkaṭalkaḷik kaṭantu cellum peṟṟiyil taṅkaḷukku oppārum mikkārumiṉṟi patiṉintām nūṟṟāṇṭu vari ciṟappāka viḷaṅkiyavarkaḷ inta yavaṉarkaḷē (arapikaḷ) avarkaḷ iyakkiya marakkalaṅkaḷ uṟutiyāṉaviyākavum kāṟṟu, nīr, veyyil, maḻi ākiyavikaḷic camāḷittuc cellum tiṟaṉuṭiyaṉavākavum iruntaṉa. perumpālum ciṟu marakkalaṅkaḷ, orē marattiṉiṉṟum kuṭintu uruvākkappaṭṭaṉa. periya kalaṅkaṉ palvēṟu marattuṇukkukaḷik koṇṭu iṇittu amikkappaṭṭaṉa. irumpu āṇikaḷik koṇṭu avikaḷi iṇippataṟkup patilāka, marap palakikaḷi oṉṟōṭoṉṟu iṇittu, avi ceyaṟkiyāka iṇikkappaṭṭaviyeṉṟu colla muṭiyātapaṭi orē cīrākac ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. tēviyāṉa pakutikaḷil tuḷikaḷi amittu marattilāṉa āṇikaḷip porutti uṟutipaṭa aḻutiṉar. kīḻkkaṇṭa vakiyiṉatāka[22] ittakiya marakkalaṅkaḷ avarkaḷāl payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa.



* * *



arapip peyar⁠ tamiḻil⁠ viparam



* * *



1.almātiyā ﻿alimōti orēmarattālāṉatu

2. attalāyā ﻿ vatti kariyōra upayōkattiṟku

3. par kāṭṭim ﻿ paṭaku kariyōra upayōkattiṟku

4. keppal ﻿ kappal neṭuṅkaṭal payaṇattiṟku

5. catri ﻿ kappal cātāraṇa kaṭalpayaṇattiṟku

6. campāṉē ﻿ cāmpāṉ ciṟu paṭaku

7. pūskā ﻿— neṭuṅkaṭal payaṇattiṟku

8. jāvā ﻿— neṭuṅkaṭal payaṇattiṟku

9. kiyāṭṭu ﻿ pariccal nāḻipōṉṟa vaṭivamippu

| 10. pārāv ||﻿ — || orē marattālāṉatu |- | 11. paṇi ||﻿ kaṭṭimaram || mīṉpiṭittalukku |- | 12. kaṅyal ||﻿ vaḷḷam || mīṉpiṭittalukku |- | 13. ṭerātā ||﻿ ... || kariyōra upayōkam |- | 14. jampukuvā ||﻿ ... || peruṅkaṭal nāvāy |}

ivikaḷit tavira, cīṉanāṭṭiṉaratu marakkala vakikaḷāṉa jaṅk, jāv, kākām pōṉṟa peruṅkappalkaḷum avarkaḷāl payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. aviyaṉittum āḻkaṭalil, peruṅkāṟṟiṉuāṭē cellattakkaṉa. āyirattiṟku atikamāṉa mālumikaḷum, payaṇikaḷum payaṇam ceyyum vakiyil ivikaḷ amikkappaṭṭaṉa. intak kalaṅkaḷiṉ utaviyāl arapiya nāṭṭiṉ teṉmuṉi siyākarasiliruntu nāṟpatu nāṭkaḷil maliyāḷak kariyil uḷḷa kollattiyum, aṅkiruntu ēḻu nāṭkaḷil tamiḻakattiṉ kiḻakkuk karip paṭṭiṉaṅkaḷiyum vantaṭiyalām. mēlum, muppatu nāṭkaḷil kaṭārattiyum (malēṣiyā) aṅkiruntu nāṟpatu nāṭkaḷil cīṉattiṉ kāṉṭaṉ nakariyum aṭiya muṭiyum. ki.pi. nāṉkām nūṟṟāṇṭilēyē arāpiyarkaḷatu kuṭiyiruppu kāṉṭaṉil iruntatu.[23] arāpiya nūlāciriyar culimāṉ, eṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭil kāṉṭaṉ nakarukkuc ceṉṟu, aṅku ērāḷamāka kuṭiyēṟi vāḻnta islāmiyar vāḻkki nili, avarkaḷ niṟi vēṟṟiya aintu nērat toḻuki, veḷḷikkiḻami ciṟapput toḻuki - ākiyavikaḷ paṟṟi kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. mēlum aṅku caṭṭatti amulākkavum, nirvākatti iyakkavum, islāmiyarkaḷi cīṉarkaḷ niyamaṉam ceytu iruntatiyum avar kuṟittuḷḷār.

iṅṅaṉam, ulakiṉ kīḻkkōṭiyāṉa﻿ cīṉattuṭaṉ toṭarpu koṇṭirunta arāpiyar, teṉṉakattiliruntu cīṉam cellum vaḻiyil kaṭāram, jāvā, cumattirā, aṉṉām ākiya valimi mikka nāṭukaḷiṉ kaṭal paṭṭiṉaṅkaḷit toṭṭuc ﻿ceṉṟaṉar. attuṭaṉ antanta nāṭukaḷiṉ celvaṅkaḷāṉa veḷḷi, īyam, takaram, paṭṭu, maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ, naṟumaṇap poruṭkaḷ, āṉittantam, kāṇṭāmiruka kompu, āmi ōṭukaḷ, pavaḷam, ampar, irumput tātukkaḷ, pāṟip paṭivaṅkaḷ, kittaṟi āṭikaḷ, karuṅkāli, cūṭaṉ maraṅkaḷ ākiyavikaḷiyum eṭuttucceṉṟaṉar.[24] intak koḷmutal poruṭkaḷukku, paṇṭamāṟṟil taṅkaḷatu contanāṭṭu necavuttuṇikaḷiyum, aṇikalaṉkaḷiyum, ulōkattakaṭṭiṉālāṉa kaṇṇāṭikaḷ, ciriyanāṭṭu kaṇṇāṭip poruṭkaḷ, kiṇṇaṅkaḷ, kuppikaḷ ākiyavikaḷi peṟṟuk koṇṭaṉar. mēlum intap poruṭkaḷip peṟṟukkoṇṭu avikaḷukku pakaramāka poṉ vaḻaṅkavum kāṉṭaṉ nakaril ki.pi. 971–l kappal niṟuvaṉam oṉṟu amikkappaṭṭatu iruntatu.[25]

nāḷaṭivil ittakiya kappal payaṇaṅkaḷ mikuntataṉ kāraṇamāka, patiṉoṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭilēyē kīḻi nāṭukaḷ aṉittilum, intiya nāṭṭiṉ pala mānilattavarkaḷum kuṟippāka kujarāttiyar, tamiḻarkaḷ – malēyā nāṭṭiṉarum kuṭiyēṟiyatuṭaṉ, cīṉattuṭaṉum, nerukkamākavaṇikat toṭarpukaḷi koṇṭiruntaṉar. eṉiṉum paṉṉāṭṭic cērnta inta vāṇikac cāttukkaḷil, palatarappaṭṭa poruṭkaḷiṉ ceṟivilum ciṟappilum arāpiyarkaḷi miñcakkūṭiyavarkaḷ evarum ilar eṉa paṉṉiraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭic cērnta ceḷa–ku–pe eṉṟa cīṉa nāṭṭu āciriyar tamatu payaṇak kuṟippukaḷil varintuḷḷār.[26] antap paṇṭaṅkaḷ aṉittum ciṟu maraṅkalaṅkaḷil mūlam kollam tuṟimukattiṟku koṇṭu varappaṭṭu aṅku iruntu peruṅkappalkaḷil niṟittu aṉuppappaṭṭaṉa. ātalāl, aṉṟiya vāṇipa ulakiṉ ciṟanta miyamāka kollam karutappaṭṭatu.

eḻuvāṉiṉ iḷaṅkatirkaḷi eṭṭip piṭikkum vakiyil kiḻakku kōṭiyiṉ pacipik peruṅkaṭaliṉ elliyit toṭṭuviṭṭa arapiyaratu antak kalaṅkaḷ, intiya camuttirattiṉ mēṟku kariyil amintirunta āppirikka nāṭṭiṉ kiḻakkup pakutikaḷukkum ceṉṟaṉa. oṉpatu pattāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil pāktātil iyaṅki vanta islāmiyap pēraraciṉ vaḷarcciyum virivum ittakiya ulakam aḷāviya vaṇika eḻuccikku uṟutimikka pīṭamāka viḷaṅkiyatu āyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar ciṟappāka viḷaṅkiya alaksāntiriyāvip pōṉṟu ulaka vāṇipattiṉ uyirnāṭiyāka appoḻutu pāktātu viḷaṅkiyatu. arāpiya varttakarkaḷ kiḻakkiyum, mēṟkiyum allāmal mattiya kaṭal pakutiyiyum taṅkaḷ vaṇika vaḻiyil iṇittaṉar. speyiṉ, cicili, ittāli ākiya nāṭukaḷukkum vaṇika vaḻikaḷ ceṉṟaṉa.[27] itaṉkāraṇamāka airōppiya nāṭukaḷ, cīṉa, intiya, āppirikka, nāṭukaḷatu kirāmpu, ēlakkāy, ampar, kastūri, karuvāyppaṭṭi, miḷaku, kaṟpūram, muttu, masliṉ, paṭṭu, cantaṉakaṭṭi ākiya poruṭkaḷip peṟṟatuṭaṉ islāmiya nāṭukaḷatu ārañcu, lemaṉ, apri kāṭ, ākiya paḻaṅkaḷum, piṉāṣ, āṭṭikōs, pōṉṟa kāykaṟikaḷ, irattiṉak kaṟkaḷ, icik karuvikaḷ, kākitam, paṭṭāṭikaḷ, ākiya poruṭkaḷiyum peṟṟaṉa. aṇmik kālaṅkaḷil ruṣyāviṉ pala pakutikaḷilum, piṉlāntu, svīṭaṉ, nārvē, piriṭṭaṉ ākiya nāṭukaḷilum akaḻntu eṭukkappaṭṭa ērāḷamāṉa islāmiyarkaḷatu nāṇayaṅkaḷ ēḻām nūṟṟāṇṭu mutal patiṉēḻāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭu variyilāṉa arāpiyaratu ciṟappumikka vāṇipac ceḻumiyip paṟi cāṟṟuvatāka uḷḷaṉa.[28] mēlum, airōppiya nāṭukaḷil arāpiyaratu vaṇika marapukaḷum, vāṇipac coṟkaḷum antanta nāṭukaḷiṉ camūka, moḻi iyalkaḷil ūṭuruvi, iṉṟuvari avi nilittu niṟkiṉṟaṉa. kuṟippāka kācōliyik kuṟikkiṉṟa arāpiyac collāṉa “ṣīk” āṅkilattil “cek” eṉa māṟṟam peṟṟuḷḷatu. itaṉip pōṉṟu jermaṉiyilum, hālantilum, kācōliyik kuṟikka arapu coṟkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttappaṭukiṟatu. “veccal” (jermaṉ) “vissaṉ” (hālantu) eṉṟa coṟkaḷ, arapuc coṟkaḷiṉ ākkam tāṉ. ivi tavira āṅkilattil vaḻaṅkappaṭukiṉṟa vāṇipam campantappaṭṭa coṟkaḷāṉa, sṭarliṅ, ṭirāpik, kēpiṉ, āvarēj, pārjasmaṉcūṉ, pārk, ṣallāp ākiya coṟkaḷ aṉittum arāpiyac coṟkaḷiṉ maṟu urukkaḷ.

﻿maṟṟum, vaṇika oppantam, vaṇikap piratinitikaḷi ayal nāṭukaḷil niyamaṉam ceytal, kaṭal elli nirṇayam, paṇṭamāṟṟu muṟikaḷ, tuṟimuka naṭimuṟikaḷ ākiya tuṟikaḷil iṉṟu muṉṉēṟiyuḷḷa airōppiya varttaka camūkattiṟku muṉṉōṭiyāka, iṉṟikku āyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉarē takka varampukaḷiyum vaḻikāṭṭu neṟikaḷiyum uruvākkiyavarkaḷ arāpiya islāmiyarkaḷ. alikaṭal āḻiyit turumpāka matittu avaṉiyi aḷaviṭap puṟappaṭṭa arāpiyarkaḷatu maṉa uṟutiyiyum ārvattiyum yārum viyantu pārāṭṭāmal irukka muṭiyātu. piṟkālaṅkaḷil puviyiyal, vāṉiyal, vaṇika iyal, kaṇita iyal, vētiyiyal, maruttuvam ākiya tuṟikaḷil ulaka makkaḷukku muṉṉōṭiyāka viḷaṅki, viññāṉa kaṇṭupiṭippu palavaṟṟiṟku vaḻikōliyavarkaḷum avarkaḷēyāvar. teṉṉakattil cōḻa pāṇṭiyarkaḷ muṭiyāṭci maṟintu vijayanakara pēraracu toṭaṅkiya kālam vari vāṇipattil ciṟappuṟṟirunta amitiyāṉa arāpiyarkaḷ, pōrccukkīciya paraṅkikaḷiṉ akkiramamāṉa, maṉitāpimāṉamaṟṟa miruka palattiṟku etirē caktiyaṟṟavarkaḷāka kaṭalvaḻiyi antak koḷḷiyariṭam viṭṭuviṭṭu āṅkāṅkē uḷnāṭṭu vāṇipattil īṭupaṭṭaṉar.

﻿iṅṅaṉam, kiḻakkiyum mēṟkiyum, taṅkaḷatu kaṭal vāṇipattāl iṇittu intiyā, ilaṅki, śrīvijayā, aṉṉām, cīṉam, ākiya kīḻi nāṭukaḷil, putiya nākarīkattiyum putiya āṉmīka oḷiyiyum pukuttiyavarkaḷ anta arāpiya muslīmkaḷ tāṉ. ātalāl, tamiḻakattiṉ ciṟappāṉa varalāṟṟukkālap pakutiyil arapiyaratu paṅkum piratāṉamāka iṭam peṟṟu iruntatu viyappukku uriyatu aṉṟu. eṉṟālum, vāṇipa nōkkuṭaṉ tamiḻakattukku vantu ceṉṟa ﻿palvēṟu, iṉa, nāṭṭu makkaḷip pōl allāmal kuṟippāka kirēkkar, urōmar, cīṉar, pāracīkattiṉarip pōl allāmal, koṇṭu vanta poruṭkaḷi viṟṟuviṭṭu, taṅkaḷ tēvikku ēṟṟa inta nāṭṭup poruṭkaḷi vāṅkic celvatuṭaṉ amiyāmal — inta nāṭṭiṉ araciyal, camutāya vāḻvil ūṭuruvi, tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ, iṇiyaṟṟa pārampariyattiṟkuriya tamiḻ makkaḷākavē māṟiyavarkaḷ, arapiyarkaḷit tavira vēṟu enta mēṟku nāṭṭiṉarum alla eṉpatē varalāṟu vaḻaṅkum teḷivāṉa ceytiyākum.

ātalāl, anta arapiyarkaḷi–tamiḻaka islāmiyarākiya avarkaḷatu toṉmiyi, tamiḻaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷ, tamiḻaka kalveṭṭukkaḷ, ceppēṭukaḷ veḷināṭṭup payaṇikaḷatu payaṇak kuṟippukaḷ ākiya varalāṟṟut taṭayaṅkaḷiṉ vāyilākat tokuttu aṟital avaciyamākiṟatu.

2

tamiḻakattil arapikaḷ





ēḻām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkam, maṉita camutāyattiṉ vaḷarcciyilē oru putiya tiruppatti tōṟṟuvittatu. teḷinta, uyarnta, teyvīka cintaṉikaḷiṉ karuvūlamāka, islām eṉṟa māperum maṟivaḻiyi, maṉita iṉam, maṇi viḷakkākak kaṇṭatu. aṉpiyum aṟattiyum ātāraṅkaḷākak koṇṭa tirumaṟiyiyum aṇṇal napikaḷ avarkaḷatu āṉmanēyak karuttukkaḷiyum, avarkaḷatu āravāramaṟṟa naṭimuṟikaḷiyum, taṅkaḷatu vāḻkki neṟikaḷākap paṟṟippiṭitta arapikaḷ, kīḻnāṭukaḷukku vaḷamiyākac cellum vaṇikarkaḷāka maṭṭumallāmal, vāḻkkikku iṉṟiyamiyāta putuvaḻiyākiya islāttip parappum iṟi nēcarkaḷākavum ceṉṟaṉar.

iṉṟiya intiya tuṇik kaṇṭattiṉ kiḻakku, mēṟku kaṭaṟkarip pakutikaḷilum, ilaṅki, malēṣiyā, intōṉiciyā, cīṉam, jappāṉ, purūṇi, pōrṉiyā, piji, pilippiṉs ākiya kīḻnāṭukaḷ aṉittilum, iṉṟu makkaḷ perum eṇṇikkiyil islāmiyarkaḷāka iruppataṟku, aṅku ceṉṟa arapiya nāṭṭu vaṇikarkaḷum toṇṭarkaḷum tāṉ kāraṇam eṉpati varalāṟṟuc ceytikaḷ viḷampukiṉṟaṉa. inta nāṭukaḷiṉ kāṭukaḷilum, malikaḷilum kaṭalōraṅkaḷilum uḷḷa nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa arāpiya iṟi nēcarkaḷiṉ aṭakkaviṭaṅkaḷ (makparākkaḷ) inta uṇmiyi eṉṟum niṉivūṭṭuṉavāka uḷḷaṉa. kuṟippāka, aṇṇal napikaḷ nāyakam (sal) avarkaḷatu neruṅkiya tōḻarkaḷāṉa tamīm-ul-aṉcāri, mukammatu ukkācā, ākiyavarkaḷatu iṟuti vāḻkki, ﻿camayat toṇṭiṟkāvē, namatu tamiḻakattil, arppaṇikkappaṭṭu iruppatum ataṟkuc cāṉṟāka avarkaḷatu aṭakkaviṭaṅkaḷ muṟiyē makamūtu pantar eṉṟa paraṅkip pēṭṭiyilum, cahītupantar eṉṟa kōvaḷattilum amintiruppatu arapunāṭṭiṟkum tamiḻakattiṟkum nalla piṇippukaḷ iruntati niṉivūṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. avarkaḷit toṭarntu pallāyiram arāpiyat toṇṭarkaḷum camayac cāṉṟōrkaḷāṉa valikaḷ, cūpikaḷ, tarvēṣkaḷ, majtūpikaḷ, mastāṉkaḷ camayap paṇikkeṉa tamiḻakattiṉ pala pakutikaḷukkum vantu cērntaṉar. toṇṭi maṇṭalattil iruntu teṉpāṇṭi nāṭuvari toṭarāka amintuḷḷa kaṭal vaḻiyāka tamiḻakattiṟkuḷ nuḻintu iṅkuḷḷa makkaḷōṭu makkaḷāka vāḻntu makkaḷatu paṇiyil taṅkaḷ vāḻkkiyi muṭakkik koṇṭa avarkaḷatu aṭakkaviṭaṅkaḷ kōvaḷattil iruntu kuḷaccal vari kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. kumari māvaṭṭattiṉ kōṭṭāṟu nakaril, īrāk nāṭṭu karsim (vali) avarkaḷatu aṭakkaviṭam uḷḷatu. atil hijri 4 (ki.pi.624) eṉṟu kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. nelli māvaṭṭattil kōtarisā maliyil uḷḷa aptul rahmāṉ (vali) avarkaḷatu aṭakkaviṭattil hijri 8 (ki.pi. 628) eṉa poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.[1] aṇṇal napikaḷār avarkaḷ kālattilēyē tirumaṟiyiṉ oḷiyi ārvattuṭaṉ tamiḻakattiṟku ēnti vanta iṟinēcarkaḷiṉ mutal aṇiyic cērntavarkaḷ ivarkaḷ eṉpatil aiyamilli. maṟṟum tiruccirāppaḷḷiyil nattar pāpā, maturiyil aliyār ṣā, ēṟupatiyil cultāṉ siyatu ipurākīm sahītu, aṉumantak kuṭiyil siyatu mukammatu pukāri: tēvipaṭṭiṉam viyatu ahamatu, ākiyōrkaḷatu tarkākkaḷ amintu iruppatu paṉṉiraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭuvari, caṉmārkka cēvikkāka arapu nāṭukaḷil iruntu tamiḻakam pōṉṟu taṅkaḷatu toṇṭiṉ muṭivil tamiḻ maṇṇil taṅkaḷi maṟittuk koṇṭa iṟinēcarkaḷil kuṟippiṭat takkavarkaḷākum.

iṉṉum, sirantip eṉa aṉṟu vaḻaṅkappaṭṭa ilaṅkiyil uḷḷa ātam﻿ (alihivasallam) avarkaḷatu tiruvaṭi maṇṇi jiyārat† [2] ceyvataṟkāka puṟappaṭṭu vanta arapikaḷatu āṉmīka toṭarpukaḷālum, islām inta maṇṇil vērūṉṟiyatu. ittakiya arapi payaṇikaḷil oruvarāṉa ṣēk aikkiyattiṉ eṉpavaratu āḻamāṉa aṉpālum, islāmiya ñāṉattālum īrkkappaṭṭa koṭuṅkōḷur maṉṉaṉ cēramāṉ perumāḷ, islātti taḻuviyatuṭaṉ ki pi. 825l puṉita hajji mēṟkoṇṭu arapi nāṭu ceṉṟavivaratti varalāṟṟu nūlkaḷ viḷampukiṉṟaṉa. nāḷaṭivil avar islātti taḻuvi, aptul rahmāṉ sāmiri eṉṟa peyaruṭaṉ arapu nāṭṭil taṅkiṉār. antap puṉita maṇṇil tamatu eñciya vāḻkkiyik kaḻittu, sapar eṉṉum nakaril ki.pi. 828-l avar iyaṟki eytiṉār.[3] inta maṉṉar vaḻaṅkiya aṟimuka maṭaluṭaṉ kēraḷam vanta mālik ipuṉu tiṉārum avaratu makkaḷum mēṟku kaṭaṟkarip paṭṭiṉaṅkaḷil islāmiya piraccāra paṇiyil īṭupaṭṭatuṭaṉ mutal muṟiyāka oṉpatu toḻukip paḷḷikaḷi anta mānilattil nirmāṇittatākavum kēraḷa mānila varalāṟṟuk kuṟippukaḷil kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa.[4]

āṉāl, ivarkaḷukku orunūṟṟāṇṭiṟku muṉṉarē tamiḻakattil, cōḻarkaḷatu kōlanakarāka viḷaṅkiya uṟiyūril hijri 116-l (ki.pi.734) hāji aptullā piṉ mukammatu aṉvar eṉpavarāl amikkappaṭṭa toḻuki paḷḷiyē teṉṉakattil islāmiyarkaḷāl nirmāṉikkappaṭṭa mutal toḻukip paḷḷiyeṉat terikiṟatu.[5] nāyaka tirumēṉiyavarkaḷ islāmiyarkaḷukkāka taṅkaḷatu tirukkaraṅkaḷāl matiṉāvil mutal toḻukip paḷḷiyi† [6] amitta oru nūṟṟāṇṭu kāla iṭiveḷiyil tamiḻnāṭṭil inta toḻukip paḷḷi amikkappaṭṭiruppatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu. tirucci nakariṉ kōṭṭi rayilniliyattiṟku arukil citinta niliyil uḷḷa antap paḷḷi, islāmiyat tamiḻiṉattiṉ muṉṉōṭi muyaṟciyākum. kaṭantu pōṉa nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil tamiḻakam vanta arapikaḷ tamiḻc camutāyattil kalantu viṭṭālum, aṉṟiya tamiḻakattiṉ mikamikac ciṟupāṉmiyiṉarāka irunta avarkaḷ taṅkaḷatu aṉṟāṭa vāḻkkiyiṉ, uṉṉatamāṉa iṟi vaḻipāṭṭiṉi niṟivēṟṟuvataṟku ettakiya uṟutiyāṉa uḷḷattuṭaṉ iruntaṉar eṉpati viḷakkum varalāṟṟu cāṭciyamāka viḷaṅkik koṇṭirukkiṟatu antap paḷḷivācal.

ivvitam, tamiḻakattil, arapi vaṇikarkaḷāṉa islāmiyarkaḷum, avarkaḷatu pōtakarkaḷāṉa iṟivaṉatu aṉupūti peṟṟa valimārkaḷum, iṟi nēcarkaḷum, kaṭaṟkarip pakutikaḷilum, paṭṭaṇaṅkaḷilum āṅkāṅkē ciṟu kuṭiyiruppu kaḷiyum amittu vāḻntaṉar. tamiḻakam pōnta ittatiya arāpiya vaṇikarkaḷatu kuṭiyiruppukaḷ caṅka kālattilum ataṟkup piṉṉarum “yavaṉac cēri” eṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu. pūmpukār nakaril,

“moḻipala perukiya paḻitīr tēyattu

puṟampeyar mākkaḷ kalantiṉi tuṟiyum...........”





ittakiya kuṭiyiruppu iruntati paṭṭiṉappāli kuṟippiṭukiṟatu. iṉṉum peruṅkāti,

“virupperuñ celvamoṭu veṉṟi tāṅkiya

aimpati ṉiraṭṭi yavaṉakcēriyum ............

(makatakāṇṭam : puṟattoṭaṅkiyatu pāṭal 3: 8)

“amariya naṇpiṉ tamaruḷum tamarām

yavaṉappāṭi āṭavar talimakaṉ”

peruṅkati - ilāvaṇakāṇṭam pāṭal. 167-168.





eṉṟu “yavaṉaccēri” “yavaṉappāṭi” eṉa teḷivākac colkiṟatu.

kiḻakku kaṭaṟkariyilum ittakiya kuṭiyiruppukaḷ cila eḻuntaṉa. mukavi māvaṭṭattu kaṭaṟkarip pakutiyi nat, vantu kuṭiyēṟiya arapu nāṭṭāriṉ nattam, pala iṅku eḻuntaṉa. iṉṟiya tēvipaṭṭiṉattiṉ teṉpakuti “arapa tāṉ kāṭu”[7] (“arapu nattakkāṭu”) eṉṟa peyaril iṉṉu vaḻaṅkappeṟukiṟatu. periyapaṭṭiṉam kirāmattiṉ vaṭakku pakutiyil irunta ittakiyatoru nattam, “nattakkāṭu” eṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭukiṟatu. kīḻakkarikku aṇmiyil uḷḷa kirāmam oṉṟu iṉṟum “nattam” eṉṟē peyar peṟṟuḷḷatu.[8]

intak kuṭiyiruppukaḷ, piṟkālak kalveṭṭukaḷilum ilakkiyaṅkaḷilum “añcu vaṇṇam” eṉṟum “añcu vaṉṉam” eṉavum kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷaṉa. kēraḷattil kuṭiyirunta muslīmkaḷatu kuṭiyiruppum "añcu vaṇṇam” eṉṟa kuṟikkapaṭṭuḷḷatu. intakkuṭiyiruppukaḷil irunta muslimkaḷ añcuvaṇṇattiṉar eṉa aḻikkappaṭṭaṉar. nāḷaṭivi avarkaḷatu capi añcuvaṇṇam cuṉṉat jamāt eṉṟum, avarkaḷatu paḷḷikaḷ añcuvaṇṇap paḷḷiyeṉṟum. vaḻaṅkappaṭṭaṉ. nelli māvaṭṭa ērvāṭi nakariṉ oru pakuti “puliyūr añcavaṇṇam” eṉṟu kalveṭṭil iṭam peṟṟuḷḷatu. kumari māvaṭṭattil “añcuvaṇṇam” eṉṟa ciṟṟuār uḷḷatu. añcumaṉ eṉṟa pāracīkac colliṉ tamiḻ vikāram tāṉ inta tamiḻp peyar eṉak karutappaṭukiṟatu.[9] maṉṟam, capi, cāttu eṉṟa poruḷiyal pāracīka moḻiyil vaḻaṅkappeṟuvatu añcumaṉ ākum. oṉpatu, pattāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ māṟavarmaṉatu tīrttāṇṭa kalveṭṭilum[10] pāskāra ravivarmaṉ eṉṟa kēraḷa maṉṉaṉatu kōṭṭayam kalveṭṭilum[11] añcuvaṇṇam kuṟikkappaṭukiṉṟatu. paḻantamiḻp pāṭal oṉṟil, nākapaṭṭiṉattukkarukil uḷḷa oru añcu vaṇṇam varṇikkapaṭukiṟatu.

“kuṭakkiṉiṟ ṟuraṅkamum vaṭakkiṉiṟ kaliṅkamum

kuṇakkiṉiṟ pacumpoṉum kuḷitta teṟkil

aṭipparap piṭikkalan taṉēka vaṇṇamāka van

tañcu vaṇṇamun taḻittaṟattiṉ vaṇṇamāṉavūr..”



eṉpatē antap pāṭaliṉ aṭikaḷākum. mēlum patiṉintāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭu ciṟṟilakkiyamāṉa palcantamāliyil “ayaṉmiku tāṉiyar añcu vaṇṇattavar” eṉṟum patiṉāṟām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattil iyaṟṟappaṭṭa mikurājamāliyil “aṇṭar taruveṉak koṭukku mañcu vaṇṇa muculimavarkaḷ” eṉa añcuvaṇṇattiṉar pēcappaṭukiṉṟaṉar.[12] itaṉip pōṉṟē nelli māvaṭṭattil cōṉakaṉ viḷiyum, mukavi māvaṭṭattil cōṉakaṉ pēṭṭiyum, tamiḻ maṇṇil taḻittu eḻunta islāmiya arapiyaratu kuṭiyiruppukaḷiyē cuṭṭuvatākum. ivi tamiḻ maṇṇil taḻittirunta poḻutum intakkuṭiyiruppu pakutikaḷiṉ muḻu nirvākattiyum islāmiyaratu cuyēṭciyāṉa āṭci amippu nirvakittu vantatu. inta amippukaḷiṉ iyakkattil inta nāṭṭu maṉṉarkaḷ taliyiṭavilli. iculāmiyaratu vāḻkki, ceyalpāṭu, kuṟṟa iyal campantamāṉa aṉittu nilikaḷilum anta taṉṉāṭci amippu naṭunāyakamāṉa talivaṉatu tīrppu kaṭip piṭikkappaṭṭatu. kāyalpaṭṭiṉattil iyaṅkiya iculāmiya taṉṉāṭci amippupaṟṟi nūlāciriyar oruvar kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.[13] “añcu vaṇṇamum taḻittu aṟattiṉ vaṇṇamāṉa ūr” eṉa mēlē taṉippāṭal kuṟippiṭuvatu inta āṭci amippiyum aṅku nilaviya “ṣariyat” muṟiyiyum eṉpatum veḷippaṭi.

ittakiya arāpiya kuṭiyēṟṟam oṉṟu. maturiyampatiyil iruntati maturi kōrippāḷiyam tarkāvil uḷḷa kalveṭṭu oṉṟu uṟuti kūṟukiṟatu.[14] itaṉip paṟṟi maturic cīmi varalāṟṟiṉ āciriyar alaksāntar nelcaṉ kuṟippiṭṭu iruppatiṉ curukkamāvatu, ... “... ... ... maturi maṉṉaṉākiya kūṉpāṇṭiyaṉ iṉṟiya kōrippāḷiyam eṉappaṭum corikuṭi, cokkikuḷam, pīpikuḷam, kaṇṇārēmpal, ciṟutūr tiruppāli ākiya āṟu pakutikaḷi, arāpiya vaṇikarkaḷukku patiṉāyiram poṉ peṟṟuk koṇṭu kiyaḷittāṉ, tamiḻ nāṭṭuk kaṭaṟkariyil ciṟappāṉa vaṇikattil īṭupaṭṭirunta avarkaḷ, maturiyil niliyākat taṅkuvataṟkāka kōrippāḷiyam eṉṟa intap pakutikaḷi pāṇṭiya maṉṉaṉiṭam iruntu peṟṟu irukkavēṇṭum. inta nikaḻcci pattāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭiṟku muṉṉatāka naṭipeṟṟu iruttal vēṇṭum. āṉāl, inta nilaṅkaḷip paṟṟiya urimip piracciṉiyoṉṟu islāmiyarkaḷukkum ēṉiyōrukkum piṉṉar eḻunta poḻutu, maturi maṉṉaṉāka irunta vīrappa nāyakkar (ki.pi. 1572-1595)﻿ niyāya pūrvamāṉa vicāraṇiyoṉṟi naṭattiṉār. kōrippāḷiyam tarkāviṟku nēril ceṉṟu āvaṇaṅkaḷi pārviyiṭṭu anta nilaṅkaḷiṉ mītāṉa islāmiyarkaḷatu urimi uṇmiyāṉateṉṟu kaṭṭaḷiyiṭṭuḷḷār. ataṉik kalveṭṭilum cālivākaṉa cakāptam 1495 pava varuṭam ti mātam patiṉōrām nāḷ(ki.pi.﻿ 1573) ﻿poṟittuḷḷār.﻿ piṟkālattil inta nilaṅkaḷi kōrippāḷiyattil eḻuntuḷḷa tarkāviṟku urimi iḻappuk kāṇiyāka arāpiya vaṇikarkaḷ viṭṭuk koṭuttirukka vēṇṭum.[15]

tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ talimi nakarāṉa maturiyil cumār āyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar arāpiya islāmiyar kuṭiyēṟṟam ēṟpaṭṭu iruntatu eṉpati mēlē kaṇṭa varalāṟṟuc ceyti veḷippaṭuttukiṟatu. itaṉip pōṉṟē cōḻar talinakarākiya tañciyilum arāpiya islāmiyarkaḷ itē kāla kaṭṭattil iruntu vantati rājarāja cōḻaṉatu tañcip periya kōyil kalveṭṭut toṭar oṉṟiliruntu cūcakamākat terikiṟatu. tañcip puṟampāṭi rājya vityātarap perunteruvil irukkum cōṉakaṉ cāvūr.....” eṉpatē antak kalveṭṭut toṭarākum.[16] rājēntira cōḻaṉatu āṭcik kālattilum celvākkuṭaṉ vāḻnta intac cōṉakari “kaṅki koṇṭa cōḻapurattu rājya vityātarap perunteru tirumantira ōli nāyakaṉāṉa cōṉakaṉ cāvūr”[17] - eṉa kōlār - kalveṭṭil kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. aracaratu vāymoḻi āṇiyi oṭṭi varintu veḷippaṭuttum aluvalar tirumantira ōli eṉappaṭum. maṟṟum, cōḻar kālattiya ciṟanta ceppu paṭṭayamāṉa “leyiṭaṉ kirāṇṭ” (āṉimaṅkalac ceppēṭu)ṭil kic cāttiṭṭavarkaḷil iṉṉoru islāmiyaratu peyar iṭam peṟṟiruppatu teriyavarukiṟatu. cattiriya cikāmaṇi vaḷanāṭṭu paṭṭaṇam kūṟṟattu caṉṉamaṅkalattu karaṇattāṉ akamatu turukkaṉ eṉa avar kuṟippiṭappaṭṭār.[18] iṉṟiya nāka paṭṭiṉamum atic cūḻnta pakuti tāṉ inta vaḷa nāṭum kūṟṟamum ākum.[19] ﻿

inta islāmiya arapikaḷi, ēṉiya veḷināṭṭiṉarip pōṉṟē tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ pākupāṭu illāmal oru kāla kaṭṭam vari “yavaṉar” eṉak kuṟippiṭṭu vantaṉa. āṉāl, ki. pi.mūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkup piṉṉar kirēkkar urōmar ākiyavarkaḷatu tamiḻakattuṭaṉ vaṇikat toṭarpukaḷ muṟṟāka muṟinta piṟaku, tamiḻakattuṭaṉ toṭarpu koṇṭirunta mēṉāṭṭār islāmiya arapiyarkaḷ maṭṭuntāṉ eṉpatu teḷivu.

itaṉi valiyuṟutta pēraṟiñar mu. rākava aiyaṅkāratu ārāyccik karuttukkaḷ iṅku oppu nōkkat takkaṉavāka uḷḷaṉa makā vitvāṉ avarkaḷ, “veḷināṭṭu viyāpārata toṭarpukaḷ” paṟṟiya tamatu kaṭṭuriyil[20]

“... ... ... ivvāṟu tamiḻnāṭṭil malintirunta yavaṉar eṉpōr, kirēkkam, arēpiyā, ekiptu mutaliya nāṭukaḷil iruntu vantavarkaḷē. ivaruḷ kirēkka yavaṉar iṅku vantu viyāpāram ceytavarāyiṉum, caṅka nāḷilum ataṉ piṉpum tamiḻakattil mikutiyākat taṅkiyavarkaḷ cōṉakar eṉṉum yavaṉarāvar. tamiḻ kūṟum patiṉeṇ tēyaṅkaḷil oṉṟāṉ “cōṉakam” parata kaṇṭattiṟku mēṟpāl nāṭukaḷil oṉṟu eṉṟu collappaṭuvatāl atu arēpiyā eṉṉum tēcamāka karutappaṭukiṟatu. apitāṉa cintāmaṇi, yavaṉar, arapiya nāṭṭu milēccar eṉa viḷakkam tantuḷḷatu. iṉṟum, kāyal paṭṭiṉam, kīḻakkari ākiya kaṭaṟkari pakutikaḷil iccōṉakar mikutiyāka vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ muṟkālattē kāviri pūmpaṭṭiṉattil vacittu vanta viyāpārak kūṭṭattic cērntavar eṉṟum, piṟkālattil pāṇṭiya nāṭu aṭintavar eṉṟum taṅkaḷ āti varalāṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷatu pūrva pāṣi arapiyāyiṉum, paṉṉūṟṟāṇṭukaḷākat tamiḻakattil taṅkiviṭṭārkaḷātalālum, tamiḻaruṭaṉ campantam ceytum, tamiḻar vaḻakkaṅkaḷip paḻaki vantamiyālum ivar tamiḻ moḻiyē pēcupavarāyiṉar, tamiḻ makkaḷ iccōṉakari yavaṉar eṉṉum peyarāl aḻittu vantaṉar ﻿eṉpatu, “cōṉakar yavaṉar” eṉpatu tivākarattālum, pattuppāṭṭil “yavaṉar” eṉṉum col varumiṭamellām nacciṉārkkiṉiyar, “cōṉakar” eṉa uri kūṟip pōntatālum viḷaṅkattakkatu.

“piṟkālattil iccōṉakar ellām tam paḻiya tēcattavar pōlavē, islātti taḻuvalāyiṉar. itu paṟṟiya “yavaṉat turukkar” eṉṟār aṭiyārkku nallār,” yavaṉar eṉṟa ilakkiyap pirayōkam islāmiya arēpiyarkaḷit tāṉ cuṭṭuvatāka makāvitvāṉ avarkaḷ mēlē kaṇṭavāṟu viḷakkam koṭuttuḷḷārkaḷ.

āciriyar peyar terintu koḷḷa iyalāta palcanta māli pāṭal “... .... ... ēḻ peruntaraṅkattu yavaṉar, allāveṉa vantu ... .... ....” eṉa, allāvit toḻukiṉṟa arapiyar, yavaṉar eṉpati aiyamaṟa aṟutiyiṭṭuk kuṟippiṭukiṟatu.[21] inta yavaṉar eṉṟa colliṉ pirayōkam patiṉmūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟku muṟpaṭṭa ilakkiyaṅkaḷil maṭṭum kāṇappaṭuvatālum, inta nūlum, anta nūṟṟāṇṭi allatu ataṟku aṭutta nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷic cārntatāka iruttal vēṇṭum. yavaṉarkaḷ taṅkaḷ vīṭṭuc cāḷaraṅkaḷukku iṭṭa mūṭutiri pōṉṟa paṭṭuttiri tirukkōyil karuvaṟikaḷila payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. ataṉik kalveṭṭukkaḷ “yavaṉiki” eṉa kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉa.[22] itēcol, piṉṉar “namaṉiki” eṉṟukūṭa vaḻakku peṟṟatu. yamaṉ eṉṟa col "namaṉ" eṉṟāl pōla, ivvitam, islāmiya arapikaḷ anta kālakaṭṭattil tamiḻakattil taṅkaḷatu islāmiya vaṇikac cāttukkaḷip perukkik koṇṭatuṭaṉ āṅkāṅkē niliyāṉa kuṭiyēṟṟaṅkaḷi amittu tamiḻccamutāyattiṉ tavirkka muṭiyāta aṅkamākiviṭṭaṉar, eṉpatum teḷivākiṟatu. taṅkaḷatu vaḷamiyāṉa vāṇipattil ﻿taṉittu niṉṟatuṭaṉ tamiḻ maṇṇukkuriya taṇṇaḷiyilum mikuntu niṉṟataṉi, atē palcantamālipiṉ iṉṉoru pāṭṭil,

"vāṉatu nāṉak koṭiyā lulaki vaḷarttaruḷum cōṉakar vāḻum ceḻum poḻil cūḻntu ...."†





[23] eṉṟu ītalaṟattiṟku ilakkaṇamāka cuṭṭappaṭum māriyē nāṉumāṟu islāmiyarkaḷatu pukaḻ vāḻkki amintu iruntatu cittarikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. iṅṅaṉam, arāpiya islāmiyarukku aṭutta tāyakamāka tamiḻ maṇ viḷaṅkiyatu. eṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ tuvakkattil irāk nāṭṭu pasrāliṉ atipatiyāka irunta hijāj ipuṉu yūcup (hijri 41 mutal 95 vari) piṉ koṭumikku añciya islāmiyar palar, kaṭal vaḻiyāka vantu pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiṉ paḻamperum tuṟiyāṉa koṟkiyil kari iṟaṅkiṉar. aṅku āṭci ceyta pāṇṭiyamaṉṉaṉ, avarkaḷi āturattuṭaṉ varavēṟṟup pukaliṭam vaḻaṅkiṉāṉ. inta akatikaḷ koṅkaṇak kariyil kuṭiyēṟiyatāka pāracīka nūlāciriyar oruvar terivittuḷḷār.[24]

itu nikaḻntatu ki.pi. 714l aṉṟu mutal islāmiyar koṟkiyil nilittu vāḻntu vantaṉar. taṅkaḷatu tāyakamāṉa keyrōvākavē (piṉṉar kāyalāṉa) koṟkiyi “kāyiṟūṉ” eṉavum “kāyiṟuṉ” eṉavum vaḻaṅkiṉar. aṅkuḷḷa ceppu paṭṭayamoṉṟil poṟittuḷḷavāṟu appoḻutu 226 cōṉakar, kāyali vantaṭintatākat terikiṟatu. avarkaḷiṉ viparam :–

1.﻿ hāṣim vamcattār ﻿ 23 pēr

nācciyār peṇṭukaḷ ﻿ 9 pēr

aṭimikaḷ ﻿ 4 pēr





2. pārūk vamcattār ⁠ 43 pēr

nācciyār peṇṭukaḷ ⁠ 16 pēr

peṇkaḷ ⁠ 9 pēr

aṭimikaḷ ⁠ 7 pēr

3. pākkīr vamcattār ⁠ 39 pēr

nācciyār peṇṭukaḷ ⁠ 24 pēr

aṭimikaḷ ⁠ 12 pēr

4. umiyā vamcattār ⁠ 14 pēr

nācciyār peṇṭukaḷ ⁠ 5 pēr

aṭimikaḷ ⁠ 5 pēr

irāṇuva vīrar ⁠ 16 pēr

kṣavarakar ⁠ 3 pēr



* * *





mottam ⁠ 226 pēr



* * *





intac ceytiyi varalāṟṟu āciriyar karṉal vilksum tamatu nūlil uṟutippaṭuttiyuḷḷār.[25] eṭṭāvatu nāṟṟāṇṭiṉ tuvakkattil īrāk āḷunarāka pasrāvil irunta hijāj piṉ kusām, hāsim kulattavari, tāyakatti viṭṭu ōṭi uyir tappum aḷaviṟku koṭumippaṭuttiyatāka anta nūlil varintuḷḷār. ivarkaḷip piṉpaṟṟi piṟitoru aṇiyiṉar, arēpiyāviṉ atipatiyāka irunta aptullā mālik piṉ marvāṉatu koṭuṅkōlukkut tappi arēpiyāviṉ teṉpakuti vaḻiyāka intiyāviṉ mēṟku, kiḻakku kaṭaṟkariyi aṭintaṉar. āṅkila kiḻakkintiyak kampeṉiyaratu iṉṉoru āvaṇattiṉpaṭi, kāyalpaṭṭiṉattil arapi muslīmkaḷ ki.pi. oṉpatāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭil, arapit tāyakattil iruntu kuṭiyēṟi, aṅku aṟupattu nāṉku toḻukip paḷḷikaḷi amittaṉar eṉṟum, teṉṉi, paṉimarat tōppukaḷ palavaṟṟi ēṟpaṭuttiṉār eṉṟum terikiṟatu. ivarkaḷum ivarkaḷatu vamcā vaḷiyiṉarum, cōḻa maṇṭalattiṉ etirk kariyāṉa īḻattuṭaṉum vaṇikat toṭarpukaḷip ﻿perukkiyatuṭaṉ, īḻattiṉ ﻿vaṭakku, vaṭakiḻakku, teṟkup pakutikaḷil – tirukkōṇamali, yāḻppāṇam, mātōṭṭam, maṉṉār, puttaḷam, koḻumpu, vēruviḷi, kāli ākiya pakutikaḷil islāmiyarkaḷatu mutalāvatu kuṭiyiruppukaḷi niṟuviṉar. ivarkaḷ īḻattiṉ muttukkaḷiyum maṇikaḷiyum peṟṟuk koṇṭu, taṅkaḷ nāṭṭu taṅkam, cempu, paṭṭu, maṭkalaṅkaḷ, kutirikaḷ ākiyavikaḷip paṇṭamāṟṟil vaḻaṅkiyatāka varalāṟṟu āciriyar nikkōlas paraṇavitāṉa kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.[26] iṉṉoru nūlāciriyarāṉa jaṭj kāci ceṭṭiyum ilaṅki muslīmkaḷippaṟṟik kuṟippiṭukiyil, avarkaḷ mutalil kumarimuṉikku kiḻakkē uḷḷa kāyalpaṭṭiṉattil, eṭṭāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭil kuṭiyēṟiya piṉṉar ilaṅkikku vantatāka varintuḷḷār. inta ūr muṉṉāl cōṉakar paṭṭiṉamāka vaḻaṅki vantatāka ṭākṭar kālṭvel kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.[27]



↑ 1. ﻿aptul rahīm em.āl.em. – islāmiya kalikkaḷañciyam (1974) tokuti﻿ 1. pakkam.

↑ jiyārat ; arapiccol iculāmiya puṉitarkaḷatu aṭakka iṭaṅkaḷukku ceṉṟu varutal eṉpatu islāmiya neṟiyil aṉumatikkappaṭṭa ceyalākum.

3

tulukkar



ivarkaḷi, piṟkālat tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ cōṉakar eṉavum, turukkar eṉavum, rāvuttar eṉavum, iṉampirittuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷaṉa. intac col tamiḻakattil patiṉōrāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭilēyē vaḻakkil iruntamikkuc cāṉṟāka patiṉōrāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭu ilakkiyamāṉa jeyaṅkoṇṭāratu kaliṅkattupparaṇi, kāñcipuram māḷikiyil, mutaṟkulōttuṅka cōḻa maṉṉaṉukku tiṟiyaḷanta nāṟpattu eṭṭut tēya maṉṉarkaḷiṉ paṭṭiyalil “turukkariyum” cērttuḷḷatu[1] atē maṉṉaṉ mītu puṉiyappaṭṭuḷḷa piḷḷit tamiḻil, kaviyaracu oṭṭakkūttarum turukkarippaṟṟiya kuṟippiṉit tarukiṟār.[2] makākavi kampaṉatu irāmāvatāramum “turukkar taravanta vayapparikaḷ ....” eṉa turukkarkaḷik kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷatu.[3] turukki nāṭṭil iruntu vanta islāmiyarkaḷ eṉṟa poruḷil intac col turukkar eṉṟum, nāḷiṭivil tulukkar - eṉavum maruvi vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷatu. intac ﻿col vaṭamoḻi, teluṅku, ilakkiyaṅkaḷilum kalveṭṭukkaḷilum “turuṣkā” eṉa payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭu uḷḷatu. “cīmatta caṉīkāra tuluska tāṉuska” eṉpatu tārāpuram kalveṭṭut toṭaril uḷḷa virutāvaḷiyākum.[4] vijaya nakara maṉṉarkaḷukku “tulukka mōkan taviḻntāṉ” “tulukka taḷa vipāṭaṉ” eṉpaṉavum, avarkaḷ virutāvaḷi (ciṟappup peyarkaḷ) eṉat teriya varukiṟatu.[5] vīrapāṇṭiya tēvaratu nilak koṭiyoṉṟil elli kuṟippiṭumpoḻutu kōvi māvaṭṭa pāriyūr kalveṭṭu, “kiḻakku puraṭṭalukku mēṟku, tulukkaṉpaṭṭi nēṟ mēṟku” eṉa variyaṟuttuḷḷatu.[6] koṅkunāṭṭil, tulukkar paṉṉiraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭil nilittuviṭṭati intak kalveṭṭu uṟuticeykiṟatu. maṟṟum tārāpuram kalveṭṭu “tulukkar paḷḷiyāki tāṉam teriyāma lākiviṭṭa[7]....” eṉṟa 14vatu nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ kalveṭṭut toṭarum “muṉṉāḷ rājarājaṉ śrīcuntarapāṇṭiyat tēvar tulukkaruṭaṉ vanta nāḷiyil ... ...” eṉṟa tirukkaḷar kalveṭṭum[8] “tulukkar palacēmaṅkaḷ tappittu ....” eṉṟa tiruvoṟṟiyūr kalveṭṭut toṭarum, tulukkar paṟṟiya varalāṟṟuc ceytikaḷi kuṟippiṭṭu, tulukkar tamiḻ maṇṇil taḻittuviṭṭa pāṅkiṉi kōṭiṭṭu kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. iṉṟikkum tamiḻnāṭṭil, cila pakutikaḷil uḷḷa ciṟṟuārkaḷ “tulukkar” kuṭiyiruppik kuṟikkum vakiyil avikaḷiṉ ūrppeyarkaḷ amintuḷḷaṉa. avi,

1. tulukkapaṭṭi — villiputtur vaṭṭam

2. tulukkapaṭṭi — cātturar vaṭṭam

3. tulukkapaṭṭi ﻿ —﻿ virutunakar vaṭṭam

4. tulukkaṉ kuḷam ﻿ —﻿ nelli vaṭṭam

5. tulukkaṉ kuḷam ﻿ —﻿ rājapāḷiyam vaṭṭam

6. tulukkaṉ kuḷam ﻿ —﻿ aruppukkōṭṭi vaṭṭam

7. tulukkaṉ kuṟicci ﻿ —﻿ mutukuḷattuār vaṭṭam

8. tulukka muttuār ﻿ —﻿ aviṉāci vaṭṭam

9. tulukka moṭṭi﻿ —﻿ kōvi vaṭṭam

10. tulukka taṇṭāḷam ﻿ —﻿ kāñci vaṭṭam



paṉṉiraṇṭāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭic cērnta tiruppattuḷar kalveṭṭil “tulukkarāyaṉ kuḻi” eṉṟa nila aḷavi kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu.[9] itiliruntu tulukkaḷil ciṟappuṭiyavar “tulukkarāyaṉ” eṉa aḻikkappaṭṭār eṉpatu pulaṉākiṟatu. mēlum, tamiḻakattil inta tulukkarkaḷatu kuṭiyiruppi kuṟikka tulukkāṇam eṉṟa putiya col vaḻakkil vantuḷḷatu. intac collum atē nūṟṟāṇṭil uruvāṉatāka irukkavēṇṭum. tirikaṭarājappakavirāyaratu “tirukkuṟṟālak kuṟavañciyilum”[10] irāmanātapuram maṉṉar “tirumali cētupati paṟṟiya vaṇṇattilum”[11] intac col āḷappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ittakiya tulukkāṇattil irunta nāyakkar oruvaratu peyaril intac col oṭṭik koṇṭuḷḷati patiṉāṟāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭu kalveṭṭu ceytikaḷil veḷippaṭuttappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avaratu peyar ēramañci tulukkāṉa nāyakkar eṉpatākum. ivar pala tiruppaṇikaḷic ceytuḷḷār. ataṉ kāraṇamāka cēlam āṟakaḻūr vaṭṭam paṇattaḷi eṉṟa ūr “tulukkāṉa nāyakkar pēṭṭi” eṉṟa putup peyaruṭaṉ vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu.[12] ﻿ivi pōṉṟē tulukkaratu āṭciyik kuṟikka “tulukkāṇiyam” eṉṟa putuc col uruvākkappaṭṭatu. patiṉāṉkāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭil maturiyic kōnakarākak koṇṭu āṭci ceyta maturi cultāṉkaḷiṉ āṭciyi “tulukkāṉiyamāka iruntu” eṉa maturit tala varalāṟṟil kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.[13] kāḷiyār kōvilil uḷḷa ki.pi. 1532 āṇṭil poṟikkappaṭṭa kalveṭṭu, tamiḻakattil nilaviya tulukkar āṭciyi “tulukka avāṇam” “tulukka avāntaram” eṉavum kuṟippiṭukiṟatu.[14] iṉṉum, “turukkar rācciyamāy pala cēmaṅkaḷ tappittu” eṉṟa tiruvoṟṟiyūr kalveṭṭut toṭarum[15] tulukkar paṟṟiya varalāṟṟuc ceytikaḷik kuṟippiṭṭu, tulukkar tamiḻ maṇṇil taḻittuviṭṭa vivaraṅkaḷi kōṭiṭṭuk kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. iṉṉum “tulukkar iṉattik” kuṟippiṭa, “tulukku” eṉṟa col kūṭa kiyāḷappaṭṭuḷḷatu. tiruvīḻimaḻali tirukkōyilil arccakar oruvar purinta aṭṭūḻiyatti tattuvap pirakācar eṉṟa pulavar, vijayanakarap pēraracarukku oru ceyyuḷ mūlamāka terivittār.

“ūḻit tulukkalla, oṭṭiyāṉ tulukkumalla

vīḻittulukku vantuṟṟatē ... ... ...”[16]





eṉpatu antak kaviyiṉ pakutiyākum.

ivvitam tulukkar eṉṟa col, “tulukku” eṉṟu maruvi piṟkālattil “malukku” eṉṟu kūṭa pirayōkam peṟiruppatip pala nūlkaḷil kāṇalām. iṉṉum, inta “turukkar”, “tulukkar” eṉṟa coṟkaḷi vērākap peṟṟu pala putiya tamiḻc coṟkaḷ tamiḻ vaḻakkiṟku vantuḷḷaṉa

avi yāvaṉa : ﻿turukkam —﻿ cellutaṟkariya iṭam, kāṭu, maliyaracaṉ

turukkam ﻿ —﻿ kastuāri, kuṅkumam

turukka vēmpu ﻿ —﻿ mali vēmpu

turukkaṟpoṭi ﻿ —﻿ cempiḷik kaṟpoṭi

turukkamāli ﻿ —﻿ kuṅkuma malarmāli

turukkattali ﻿ —﻿ karu niṟamuḷḷa kaṭal mīṉ vaki

tulukki ﻿ —﻿ ciṅkāri

tulukkaṭuvaṉ ﻿ —﻿ oruvaki nel

tulukkappū ﻿ —﻿ tulukkac cevvanti

tulukka malliki ﻿ —﻿ piḷḷiyār pū eṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭum malar, ceṭi

tulukka pacaḷi ﻿ —﻿ kīri vaki

tulukka payaṟu ﻿ —﻿ payaṟu vaki

tulukka kaṟṟāḻi ﻿ —﻿ kariya pavaḷam (nāṭṭu maruntu)



maṟṟum, “turukkarnāṭu” eṉṟa nilakkūṟu iruntati patiṉmūṉṟāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭu ilakkiya karttāvāṉa parañcōti muṉivar, tamatu tiruviḷiyāṭal purāṇattil kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷatum[17] iṅkuporuttamuṭiyatāka uḷḷatu. maṟṟum, tamiḻil, ṣarpat, cippāy, maṇaṅku, tarpār, tikkā vakkīl, amīr, ulamā, kāji, jākir, jamīṉtār pōṉṟa turukki moḻic coṟkaḷum, tamiḻc coṟkaḷāka vaḻakkiṟku vantuḷḷaṉa.



↑ pulavar jeyaṅkoṇṭār kaliṅkattuparaṇi pāṭal 333 vattalar, mattikar, māḷuvar

mākatar maccar milēccarkaḷē

cūttirar kuttar kuṭakkar

piṭakkar kurukkar turukkar”

4

cōṉakar





﻿ivvitam tamiḻakam pōnta islāmiya arapukaḷi turukkar eṉa aḻippatuṭaṉ cōṉakar eṉa aḻikkum vaḻakkamum vantatu. villiyam lōkāṉ eṉṟa āṅkila nūlāciriyar, yavaṉaka eṉṟa coltāṉ cōṉakarāki viṭṭatāka karuttu terivittuḷḷār.[1] “yōṉā” eṉṟa pirākirutac colliṉ mūlamāka piṟantatu cōṉa eṉṟum, attuṭaṉ tamiḻ “ka” vikuti cērntu “cōṉaka” eṉa tamiḻ vaṭivam peṟṟuḷḷatu eṉa tamiḻaka tolporuḷ tuṟi iyakkunar ṭākṭar irā. nākacāmi avarkaḷum kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷārkaḷ.[2] āṉāl ki.mu. mūṉṟāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭil irunta cōṉakar eṉṟa peḷatta pikkuviṉ kuṟippi makāvamcattil paṭikkumpoḻutu, pāli moḻi intac collukku mūlamāka irukkalām eṉpatum cintikkattakkatāka uḷḷatu.[3] ātiyil intac col eṭṭāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭu mutal iculāmiya arapiyarkaḷit tāṉ kuṟittu vantuḷḷatu. poṉṉukku “cōṉa” (corṇa) eṉṟa peyarum uṇṭu. “poṉṉoṭu vantu kaṟiyōṭu peyarum” paṇṭa māṟṟu muṟiyil poṉṉik koṇṭuvantu, tamiḻaka ki viṉip poruṭkaḷi peṟṟuc ceṉṟa kāraṇattiṉālum avarkaḷ cōṉakar eṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭṭu irukkalām. patiṉoṉṟāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭu mutal, intaccol tamiḻ vaḻakkil iruntati rājarāja tēvaṉatu tañcip peruvuṭiyār kōvil kalveṭṭukkaḷ uṟuti ceykiṉṟaṉa, “cōṉakaṉ cāvūr” eṉpavarum “cōṉaka ciṭukku” eṉṟa aṇiyum kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu.[4] maṟṟum, anta maṉṉaṉatu patiṉintāvatu āṭciyāṇṭu kalveṭṭu "cōṉaka vari" paṟṟiya kuṟippit tarukiṟatu.[5] inta māmaṉṉaṉatu mintaṉāṉa irājēntira cōḻaṉatu kōlār kalveṭṭil, “tirumantira ōli nāyakaṉ kaṅkikoṇṭa cōḻapurattu rājya vittiyātarap perunteru cōṉakaṉ cāvūr” eṉpavari kuṟippiṭukiṟatu. maturip pāṇṭiyaratu vāricup pōril parākkirama pāṇṭiyaṉukku utava vanta ciṅkaḷap paṭiyiṉi, irāmanātapuram māvaṭṭam cempoṉmāri arukil, ki.pi. 1170l, paricup poruṭkaḷuṭaṉ aṅkirunta cōṉakar varavēṟṟu makiḻntatāka ilaṅki varalāṟṟu nūlāṉa makāvamcatti mēṟkōḷkāṭṭi pērāciriyar es. kiruṣṇacāmi aiyaṅkār varintuḷḷār.[6] intac cempoṉmārikku aṇmiyil arapikaḷatu añcuvaṇṇam amintiruntati oruvāṟu ūkikka muṭikiṟatu.

pāṇṭiyar kalveṭṭukkaḷil iruntum, tamiḻakattil cōṉakar paṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷ teriya varukiṉṟaṉa. pāṇṭiyaratu meykkīrttikaḷil, pala nāṭṭavarkaḷil oruvarāka, cōṉakar collappaṭṭu irukkiṉṟaṉar.

"kuccararum āriyarum kōcalarum koṅkaṇarum

vaccirarum kāciyarum mākatarum

arumaṇarum cōṉakarum avantiyaru mutalāya ... ...”



eṉpatu caṭiyavarmaṉ cuntarapāṇṭiyaṉ (ki.pi. 1258 - 71) meykīrttiyiṉ oru pakutiyākum.[7] pāṇṭiyaṉ tirupuvaṉac cakkaravartti cuntarapāṇṭiyaṉatu kalveṭṭu oṉṟil “cōṉakaṟku cīvitamāka aṭinta nāḷil” eṉa avarkaḷ pāṇṭiya nāṭṭil pukaliṭam peṟṟatik kuṟippiṭukiṟatu.[8] pāṇṭiyaṉ kōṉēriṉmi koṇṭaṉātu tiruppullāṉik kōyil kalveṭṭu, “pavuttira māṇikkap paṭṭiṉattu cōṉaka cāmantap paḷḷikku” cila ūrkaḷi iṟiyiliyāka vaḻaṅkappaṭṭa āṇiyit terivikkiṟatu.[9]

itaṉi, antanta nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil eḻunta tamiḻ ilakkiyak kuṟippukaḷum, uṟutippaṭuttukiṉṟaṉa. patiṉintāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭu islāmiya ilakkiyamāṉa palcantamāli, cōṉakar tamiḻaka vāṇipattil vaḷarntōṅki viḷaṅkiyatuṭaṉ, tamiḻ maṇṇukkuriya taṇṇaḷiyilum, tāḷāṇmiyilum mikku niṉṟatic cuṭṭik kāṭṭukiṟatu.

“vāṉatu nāṇak koṭiyālulaki vaḷart taruḷum

cōṉakar vāḻum ceḻumpoḻil .... ....”



eṉa ītalaṟttiṟku ilakkaṇamāṉa māriyi nāṇumāṟu islāmiyaratu koṭi vāḻkki amintu iruntatik kōṭiṭukiṟatu.[10] ittaku ceḻumiyāṉa camutāyattil, piṟkālattil īyntu civanta irukarattu cītakkāti vaḷḷal tōṉṟiyatil viyappu illitāṉ. paṉṉiraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭil vāḻntavarāṉa perumpaṟṟap puliyūrār tamatu tiruvālavāyuṭiyār purāṇattil cōṉakari kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.[11] patiṉmūṉṟāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭu pērilakkiyamāṉa irāmāyaṇam - cuntarakāṇṭam ūrtēṭu paṭalattil cōṉakar maṉi paṟṟiya kuṟippu uḷḷatu.[12] patiṉāṉkāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭu uri āciriyarāṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyar, pattuppāṭṭil yavaṉar eṉṟa collukku cōṉakar eṉṟē poruḷ pirittuḷḷār. ivari aṭuttu vāḻnta parañcōtiyārum namatu tiruviḷiyāṭal purāṇattil tirumaṇappaṭalattil cōṉakar paṟṟi pāṭiyuḷḷār.[13] atē kālavariyil paṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷa cūṭāmaṇi, tivākara nikaṇṭukaḷil cōṉakarukku viḷakkam variyappeṟṟuḷḷatu. tamiḻmoḻi vaḻaṅkiya patiṉeṭṭu tēcaṅkaḷil cōṉakamum oṉṟu eṉa naṉṉūlil āciriyar pavaṇantiyār kuṟittuḷḷār. ivar patiṉmūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭil vāḻntavar. ceṉṉi palkalikkaḻaka pērarakarātiyāṉa lekṣikaṉ “cōṉakam” intiya tuṇik kaṇṭattiṟku mēṟkē uḷḷa arēpiyā, pāracīkam nāṭukaḷ eṉa teḷivupaṭuttiyuḷḷatu. cilappatikāra uriyāciriyarāṉa aṭiyārkku nallār, paḻantamiḻnāṭṭil, kuravikkūttu, āycciyar kūttu pōṉṟu, “cōṉaka kūttu” eṉa oruvakik kūttu naṭikkappaṭṭati tamatu uriyil varintuḷḷār. ataṉi “tuñcāta ammip pūc cōṉaka mañcari” eṉak kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. kaṭaṟkarip paṭṭiṉaṅkaḷāṉa toṇṭi, maṇṭapam, vētāḷi, kīḻakkari, tūttukkuṭi, kāyalpaṭṭiṉam, kaṭalūr ākiya ūrkaḷil vaḻakkil uḷḷa “cōṉakar teru”, eṉṟa pakutikaḷ, paḻantamiḻarkaḷatu kuṭiyiruppukkaḷiṉiṉṟum ivarkaḷatu maṉikaḷi vēṟupaṭuttik kāṭṭuvataṟkākavē eḻuntaviyākum. avi pōṉṟē, avarkaḷatu kuṭiyiruppukaḷil cila, avarkaḷatu toṉmiyic cuṭṭum vakiyil “cōṉakaṉ pēṭṭi” (irāmanātapuram māvaṭṭam) “cōṉakaṉ viḷi” (nelli māvaṭṭam) eṉṟa paḻampeyarkaḷuṭaṉ iṉṟum vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa. itaṉi "neytal cārnta marutattil nērvār tulukkar, cōṉakarām” eṉa pirapanta tiraṭṭu cuṭṭiyuḷḷatu.[14] atē nūlil iṉṉoru pāṭal, paccimali, karuppāṟu, vaccira vaḷanāṭu, kāyalpaṭṭiṉam, ūr māli, kuṅkumam, pacumpuravi, veḷḷiyāṉi, ciṅka koṭi ākiyavi cōṉakarukku uriyaṉa eṉpatākak kuṟippiṭukiṟatu.[15]

maṟṟum “cōṉaka vāḷi”, “cōṉaka keḷutti” eṉṟa mīṉ vakikaḷip paṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷ pērakarātiyāṉa lekcikaḷil koṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.[16] iṉṉum “cōṉakac ciṭukku” eṉṟatoru aṇiyum tamiḻ makaḷiratu aṇikalaṉkaḷil oṉṟāka iruntati tañci peruvuṭiyār kōvil kalveṭṭukkaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.[17] ivikaḷum, ivi pōṉṟa ēṉiya varalāṟṟu, ilakkiyat taṭayaṅkaḷum, vaṇikattiṟkāka arapu nāṭukaḷil iruntu tamiḻakam pōnta islāmiyar inta maṇṇiṉ nalla marapukaḷukku iyintu islāmiyat tamiḻarkaḷāki, tulukkarāki, piṉṉar cōṉakar eṉṟa putuc collālum vaḻaṅkappaṭṭu vantaṉar eṉpati orumukappaṭutti uṟuti colvataṟku utavukiṉṟaṉa. ivvitam cōṉakar tamiḻccamutāyattiṉ pala tuṟikaḷilum kalantu tamiḻarkaḷākavē māṟiviṭṭa poḻutilum āntira mānilattil rājarāja cōḻaṉatu āṭcippakutiyāṉa tirukkāḷattiyil nilittu irunta cōṉakarkaḷiṭamiruntu “cōṉakavari” vacūlikkappaṭṭa ceytiyum namatu varalāṟṟil uḷḷatu.

5

rāvuttar





pattām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyil eḻuntu, patiṉoṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭil parantuvirinta, cōḻap pēraraciṉ nerukkam kāraṇamāka teṉṉakattiṟkum arāpiya nāṭukaḷukkumiṭiyē nikaḻnta paṇṭamāṟṟu atuvari illāta aḷaviṟku viṟuviṟuppi eṭṭiyatu. arāpiya pāracīka nāṭṭuk kutirikaḷ atil piratāṉa iṟakkumatiyāka iruntatu. kaṭiccaṅka nūlāṉa paṭṭiṉappāli, "nīriṉ vanta nimir parippuravi" niṟinta pūmpukār tuṟiyic cittarikkiṟatu.[1]

“viḻumiya nāvāy peru nīrōccunar

naṉintavi tēettu naṉkalaṉuymmār

puṇarntuṭaṉ koṇarnta puraviyō ṭaṉittum

vikaṟoṟum vaḻi vaḻi ciṟappu ... ...”



eṉṟu maturi kāñci, puravikaḷ vanta vaḻiyi ciṟappittu colkiṟatu.”[2]

“taṟukaṇ āṇmiya, tāmari niṟattaṉa, taki cāl

maṟuvillāṉ kuḷam puṭiyavaṉ ... ...”



eṉṟum,

“pīḷa mā mayilerut taṉṉap peruvaṉappuṭiyaṉa

māli mā rāṭṭat takattaṉa vaḷariḷaṅ kiḷiyē

pōtu mēṉiya ... .... ....”



[3]

﻿eṉak kutiriyiṉ aḻakil maṉati paṟikoṭuttup pāṭukiṟār civakacintāmaṇi āciriyar.

kutirikaḷ, tamiḻnāṭṭiṟkup putumiyāṉavi alla. kutirikaḷiṉ vaṇṇam, vaṭivu, taṉmi ākiya iyalpukaḷik koṇṭu avikaḷukku, pāṭalam, kōṭakam, ivuḷi, vaṇṇi, pari, kantukam, puravi, kaṉavaṭṭam, turakam, kaṟki, accuvam, turaṅkam, yavaṉam, kurakatam, viyāḷi eṉa pala peyarkaḷ tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷil cūṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iṉṉum avikaḷiṉ uṭaṟkūṟu ilakkaṇaṅkaḷiyum variyaṟuttu pala nūlkaḷil virivākak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. parañcōti muṉivar, tamatu perunūlāṉa tiruviḷiyāṭaṟ purāṇattil pala vakiyāṉa parikaḷiyum, avikaḷiṉ iyalpukaḷiyum avikaḷ ententa nāṭukaḷik cārntavi eṉpatiyum vivaramākak kuṟippiṭuvatuṭaṉ, "yavaṉam" eṉṟa vakip puravi makkattil uḷḷavi eṉa pākupaṭutti pāṭiyuḷḷār. kaṭamparkaḷum pallavarkaḷum taṅkaḷatu āṭciyil kutirikaḷi nampi iruntatāka pērāciriyar nīlakaṇṭacāstirikaḷ varintuḷḷār.[4] toṉṟutoṭṭu, tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ nālvakip paṭip pakuppil kutirippaṭiyum oru pirivāka iruntu vantuḷḷati yāvarum aṟivar. aṟupattu nāṉku kalikaḷil kutiriēṟṟamum oṉṟāka iruntatu. pōril kutiriyiṉ maṟattip paṟṟip pāṭuvataṟkāṉa tuṟiyoṉṟu vakukpaṭṭiruntati puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmāli cuṭṭukiṉṟatu. pattāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭil, cōḻap pēraracup perukkattiṟku' kutirikaḷ perumaḷavil tēvippaṭṭaṉa. inta uṇmiyi antak kālattu ilakkiyaṅkaḷāṉa kaliṅkattupparaṇi, kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉ piḷḷit tamiḻ, mūvarulā, muttoḷḷāyiram ākiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷil cōḻarkaḷiṉ veṟṟiyuṭaṉ kutirikaḷ paṟṟiya pukaḻcciyum puṉintu āṅkāṅkē kuṟippiṭappaṭukiṟatu. pāracīka, arapunāṭukaḷil iruntu, cōḻanāṭṭil kutirikaḷ iṟakkumati ceyyappaṭṭa poḻutu arapiya muslīmkaḷum tamiḻakattiṟku uṭaṉ varavēṇṭiya nirppantamum ēṟpaṭṭatu. toṭakkattil, kutirikaḷ perumpālum koṅkaṇa, kēraḷak﻿karip paṭṭiṉaṅkaḷil kari iṟakkappaṭṭu, koṅku nāṭṭu vaḻiyāka cōḻa nāṭṭiṟkuḷ koṇṭu varappaṭṭaṉa. iṉṉum, kōḻikkōṭu nakaril kutiri vaṭṭam eṉṟa pakutiyum, kōyamputtūr māvaṭṭattil kutirippāḷiyam eṉṟa ūrum iruppatu iṅku niṉivu kūrattakkatu. ittakiya vaṇikattil īṭupaṭṭuḷḷavarkaḷi “kutiric ceṭṭikaḷ” eṉa kalveṭṭukkaḷum ilakkiyaṅkaḷum kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉa.[5]

arapukkuṭā nāṭṭuk kutirikaḷiṉ naṭamāṭṭamum vaṇikamum, piṟkālap pāṇṭiya pēraracu kālattilum toṭarntu viṟuviṟuppāka naṭipeṟṟaṉa. āṇṭutōṟum, patiṉāyiram kutirikaḷ pāṇṭiya nāṭṭup peruntuṟikaḷāṉa kāyalpaṭṭiṉam, tēvipaṭṭiṉam, ākiya tuṟikaḷil kari iṟakkappaṭṭaṉa.[6] aṅkiruntu, avi muṟiyē nellikkum maturikkum naṭatti, kaṭattic cellappaṭṭaṉa. anta vaḻikaḷil oṉṟu iṉṉum mutukuḷattuār vaṭṭattil “kutiri vaḻikkāṭu” eṉṟu vaḻaṅkappaṭukiṟatu. anta vaḻiyit toṭarnatu tiruccentūr paramaṉkuṟicciyil, “kutiri vaḻikkuḷam” iruntu varuvatu ārāyattakkatu. intak kutirikaḷi pāṇṭiyaṉukkākap peṟṟuvarac ceṉṟa vātavūrar, tirupperuntuṟiyil tiruppaṇi ceytu tīṭci, peṟṟu māṇikkavācakarāṉa katiyi tirup peruntuṟippurāṇam, “kōṭṭamilā māṇikkavācakar muṉ kutiri rāvuttaṉāka” iṟivaṉ vantu" niṉṟatākak kuṟippiṭukiṟatu.[7] anta tirupperuntuṟiyil uḷḷa ālayattiṉ mukappu maṇṭapam, kutiri vīrar ciṟpaṅkaḷuṭaṉ amikkappaṭṭu iruppatuṭaṉ, “rāvuttar maṇṭapam” eṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭutalum īṇṭu kuṟippiṭattakkatu. maturi tiruviḷiyāṭal purāṇattil, parañcōti muṉivar, kutiri vaṇikarāka vanta kūṭalmānakara iṟivaṉi,

“iṉṉiṟi maṉṉar maṉṉar



iṉimi karntu irāvuttaṟku”



naṉmi kūr varicit tūcu

nalkumeṉṟu ... ..." eṉavum,





perumpaṟṟappuliyūrār

“tuyya pērūliṟ kellām tulaṅkiya rāvuttarāyaṉ” eṉavum pāṭukiṉṟār.[8] patiṉintu nūṟṟāṇṭu ilakkiyaṅkaḷāṉa kaliveṇpāvilum, kantar alaṅkārattilum aruṇakirinātar tām vaḻipaṭum murukavēḷi,

“cūrkkoṉṟa rāvuttaṉē! .... .... ....”

“māmayilēṟum rāvuttaṉē! .... .... ....”





eṉa kutiri rāvuttaṉākavē kaṟpaṉi ceytu pāṭiyuḷḷār.[9] pāracīkak kuṭā nāṭukaḷāṉa, kiṣ, hermus, kurōṣāṉ, kāttip, lahsā ākiya iṭaṅkaḷiliruntu āṇṭoṉṟukku aiyāyiram mutal patiṉāyiram kutirikaḷ pāṇṭiyap pēraracaṉāl taravaḻikkappaṭṭaṉa veṉṟum ovvoru kutirikkum irunūṟṟu irupatu tiṉār (cempoṉ) vaḻaṅkappaṭṭateṉṟum varalāṟṟu āciriyar vassāp varintuḷḷār.[10]

ivikaḷil, pāracīka nāṭṭil iruntu varappeṟṟa kutirikaḷ “pari” eṉṟum, turukki nāṭṭuk kutirikaḷ "turakam" eṉṟum, mattiya āciyā korāsaṉ pakuti kutirikaḷ “kōram” eṉṟum vaḻaṅkappaṭṭaṉa.[11] ivikaḷilum, cēramaṉṉar kutiri "pāṭalam" eṉṟum cōḻaṉatu kutiri "kōram" eṉṟum pāṇṭiyatu pari "kaṉavaṭṭam" eṉṟum ilakkiyaṅkaḷil kuṟippiṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. "kōrattuk koppō kaṉavaṭṭam ammāṉi ...” eṉpatu kaviyaracar oṭṭakkattar





“arapu nāṭṭuk kutirikaḷ patināṉkām nūṟṟāṇṭil tamiḻakam varutal”

(tiruppiṭimarutuār kōyil kōpura vaṇṇa oviyam)

vākku.[12] kutiriyik kuṟikkum “pari” eṉṟa collum FAR eṉṟa pāracīkac coltāṉ. inta tici coṟkaḷi oṭṭi kutiri campantamāṉa cēṇam (Cenam), lakāṉ (Lakan) cavukku (Cavukka) cavāri (Safary) eṉṟa pāracīka coṟkaḷ tamiḻil kalantu tamiḻākavē vaḻakkil uḷḷaṉa. iṉṉum kaṭivāḷam, mokkaṉi eṉpaṉa ittaku toṭarpuṭiya tamiḻ vaḻakkākum. arapu nāṭṭukkutirikaḷum arapiyarum perum marakkalaṅkaḷil namatu kaṭaṟkarikku vantu cērum nikaḻcciyi vaṇṇa oviyamāka tiruppuṭi marutūr kōpurataḷattil tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu.[13] anta oviyatti uṟṟu nōkkiyapiṉ intappāṭalip paṭittuppāruṅkaḷ’’.

“iruṅkaḻic ceṟuliṉ tīmpuḷi veḷḷuppu

parantōṅku varippiṉ vaṉṉakat timilar

koḻumīṉ kuṟikiya tuṭikkaṭ ruṇiyal

viḻumiya nāvāy perunīrōc cunar

naṉantali tēettu naṉkala ṉuymmār

puṇarntuṭaṉ koṇarnta puravi yōṭaṉittum...”



— maturi kāñci 8–16:26



itaṉi orumuṟi mīṇṭum paṭittu viṭṭu ōviyatti uṟṟu nōkkuṅkaḷ, intap pāṭali appaṭiyē tūrikiyāl tiṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu pōṉṟu teriyavarum. inta ōviyam patiṉāmkām nūṟṟāṇṭic cērntatākum. intak kutiri vaṇikam iru nāṭukaḷiṉ uṟavu niliyil oru putiya tiruppatti ēṟpaṭuttiyatuṭaṉ, tamiḻakattil pallāyiram arāpiya illāmiyar vantu taṅkuvataṟkāṉa poṉṉāṉa vāyppum vāḻkki niliyiyum utaviṉa. tamiḻ maṇṇil vantu iṟaṅkiya arapik kutirikaḷip paḻakki, tamiḻnāṭṭu muṟiyil payiṟci aḷittu varavum, avikaḷukku ēṟṟa uṇavu vakikaḷi koṭuttup parāmarikkavum, vittiya utavi vaḻaṅkavum, arapikaḷatu paṇi tēvippaṭṭatu. kutiri vaḷarppil paḻakkamum tiṟamiyum mikkavarkaḷ aṉṟiya tamiḻakattil aritāka iruntaṉar.[14] patiṉintām nūṟṟāṇṭu ﻿vari toṭarnta inta vaṇikattip paṟṟi pala varalāṟṟu āciriyarkaḷ vivaramāka varintuḷḷaṉar. inta nūṟṟāṇṭu iṟutiyil pāracīka nāṭṭil iruntu kutiri viyāpārikaḷāka vanta cultāṉ kuli eṉpavar ki.pi. 1518l aitarāpāt camastāṉattil (āntirāvil) kutup ṣāuṟi parampari ēṟpaṭuttiyavar eṉpatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu.[15] attuṭaṉ intak kutiri vaṇikattāl tamiḻnāṭṭu aracu varuvāy iṉaṅkaḷil mūṉṟu putiya iṉaṅkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. avi, kutiri vari, kutirik kāṇikki, kutirip panti eṉpaviyākum.[16] pāṇṭiyarkaḷatu āṭciyiṉ muṭivu vari amulil iruntati pala kalveṭṭukkaḷil iruntum ceppēṭukaḷil iruntum terintu koḷḷa muṭikiṟatu. maṟṟum nāyakkarkaḷatu, āṭciyiṉ poḻutu "kutirik koṭi" eṉṟa ciṟappum vaḻakkil iruntatu, teriyavarukiṟatu.[17] ivvitam oru kāla kaṭṭattil tamiḻaka araciyalil piratāṉa aṅkamāṉa arapu nāṭṭuk kutirikaḷ viḷaṅkiyatuṭaṉ ataṉik koṇarntu paḻakki, pēṇi, tamiḻ maṉṉarkaḷukkup payaṉpaṭac ceytu arapikaḷ, rāvuttarkaḷeṉa peyar peṟṟatum tamiḻarkaḷuṭaṉ maṇa uṟavu koṇṭu tamiḻk kuṭikaḷākavē māṟiviṭṭatu, namatu varalāṟṟil ciṟappāṉa amcamākum.

toṉmik kālaṅkaḷil, tamiḻaka islāmiya arapikaḷukku inta ciṟappup peyar vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu. nāḷaṭivil kutiri vaṇikam maṭṭumallāmal, aracu cēviyil kutiri vīrarākavum, kutiri aṇiyiṉ taḷapatiyāka viḷaṅkiyavarkaḷik kuṟikkavum intac col payaṉpaṭṭuḷḷatu. tirupperuntuṟiyil, kutiri vaṇikarāka vantu, taṉatu tuyarkaḷinta iṟivaṉi, "tuyya pērulakiṟkellām tulaṅkiya rāvutta rāyaṉ" eṉa vāyāra, vāḻttukiṟār vātavūr aṭikaḷ. irāmappiyaṉ ammāṉi kutiriyaṇi taḷapatiyi “rāvutta karttaṉ” eṉa kuṟippiṭukiṟatu,[18] “rāvuttarāyaṉ” “rāvuttakarttaṉ” eṉṟa vaḻakkukaḷum islāmiyat tamiḻarkaḷic cuṭṭuvataṟkāka eḻunta coṟkaḷākum. intac coṟkaḷukkāṉa vēr. enta moḻiyil oṭṭiyuḷḷatu eṉpatu āyvukkuriya toṉṟākum. rāpittū eṉṟa arapic col. tamiḻil vikāramaṭinta “rāvutta” eṉṟa vaḻakku peṟṟu iruppatāka cilar karutukiṉṟaṉar. etiriyi etirkka cittamāka iruppavaṉ eṉpatē rāpittuḷ. tamiḻil maṭṭumallāmal, “rāvuttar” teluṅku moḻiyilum “rāvut” eṉac caṟṟu kuṟukalāṉa vaṭivil vaḻakkil iruntu varukiṟatu. kutiri vīraṉ eṉṟa poruḷil, “immāṭi rākuttarāyaṉa” “rākuttarāyaṉ ciṅkappa nāyakkaṉ” pōṉṟa teluṅku maṉṉarkaḷatu peyarkaḷ tiruppati – tirumali kalveṭṭukkaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.[19] īrōṭu kalveṭṭu oṉṟu, teluṅku āḷunarāṉa “parvata rākuttaṉ” eṉpavarik kuṟippiṭukiṟatu.[20] rājarāja cōḻaṉatu virutukaḷil, “rākuttamiṇṭaṉ” eṉṟa collum vaḻaṅki vantuḷḷatu,vaṭāṟkāṭu māvaṭṭam, kāvērippākkam kalveṭṭukkaḷil ki. pi. 1509l ciṅkaya rāvuttaṉ taṅkal eṉṟa kirāmamum ki. pi. 1530l kāmāṭci rāvuttaṉ taṅkal eṉṟa kirāmam kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.[21] cēlam māvaṭṭam āṟakaḻur kalveṭṭu oṉṟil hoycāḷa rāmatēvaratu patiṉmūṉṟāvatu āṭciyāṇṭil, nāṭṭa maṅkalam eṉṟa kirāmam rākutta rāyaṉ iṟiyili tēvatāṉamāka" vaḻaṅkappaṭṭa ceyti kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu.[22] maturi cokkēcar ālayattiruppaṇi ceytavarkaḷil timmu rāvuttar eṉpavar kuṟippiṭappaṭukiṟār.[23]avaratu kālam ki. pi. 1564 eṉṟum teriya varukiṟatu. avarit “taṇṭamiḻk kacci vaḷampati vāḻuñ caturaṉ” eṉṟum “tiṇṭaruṅ kīrtti mikka cuvap piyaṉ timmu rāvuttaṉē” eṉṟum pukaḻntu urippavi tiruppaṇi māliyil uḷḷa toṭarkaḷ. ﻿perumpālāṉa muslīmkaḷ, iṉṟum “rāvuttar” eṉṟa vikutiyi taṅkaḷ peyaruṭaṉ cērttuk koḷvatu pōla tamiḻ nāṭṭil uḷḷa teluṅku moḻi pēcum nāyakkarkaḷil ciṟu oru piriviṉar taṅkaḷ peyaruṭaṉ “ravat” eṉṟa vikutiyiyum iṇittu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. teṉṉakattil nilavum cātikaḷiyum kuṭikaḷiyum muḻumiyāka āyvu ceytu pala tokutikaḷāka tamatu āyviṉi veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷa āciriyar eṭkār tarsṭaṉ, rāvuttar eṉpavarkaḷ, islāmiya makkaḷāṉa leppi, marakkāyar, maṟṟum cōṉakar payaṉpaṭuttum virutuppeyar eṉṟē kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.[24] piṟkāla ilakkiyamāṉa pirapanta tiraṭṭu, tamiḻakattil vacittu varum palvēṟu cātikaḷil rāvuttarum oru piriviṉar eṉa tokuttuḷḷatu.[25]

taṅkaḷatu toṉmiyi ivvitam niṉivu kūṟum vakiyil tamiḻaka rāvuttar iṉṟum maṇaviḻākkaḷil poḻutu, maṇamakaṉi naṉku alaṅkarittu kutirimītu ēṟṟi vittu, maṇamakaḷ illattiṟku aḻittuc cellum vaḻakkam uṭiyavarkaḷāka irukkiṉṟaṉar. intappaḻakkam, tamiḻaka islāmiyarkaḷiṭiyē nāṉūṟu āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉarum iruntu vantatu eṉpati perum pulavar umaṟuviṉ pāṭalkaḷil iruntu pulappaṭukiṟatu. iṟimaṟiyi veḷippaṭutti islāttip parappiya ēntal napi nāyakam avarkaḷatu vāḻkkiyi vivarikkum cīṟāppurāṇa kāviyam, tamiḻ nāṭṭil iyaṟkip piṉṉaṇi, paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷ, marapukaḷ ākiyavikaḷi uḷḷaṭakkiyatāka puṉiyappaṭṭu uḷḷatu. maṇamakaṉākiya mukammatunapi (sal) avarkaḷ maṇakkōlattuṭaṉ pariyil amarntu pavaṉiyāka maṇamakaḷ illam ceṉṟati,

“ tāviya pari mēṟ cēṉit taḷat toṭum vīti vāyiṉ

mēviya vaḷḷalār tam mey eḻil nōkki ... ...”



“ kaṭunaṭip puravi mēlāy kaviki māniḻaṟṟa vanta

vaṭivuṭi mukammatiṉ taṉ vaṉappalāl vaṉappu milli .... ..”





﻿ ⁠“tiriyiṉṟi piṟanta moḻiyār ceḻumaṉit tīpaṅ kaḷēnta

﻿ ⁠irupuṟa neruṅki ayiṉinīr cuḻaṟṟa veṇṇila ālatti eṭuppa

﻿ ⁠pāviyiṉ maṟiyil kuraviyuñ cilampa pariyi viṭṭiraṅkiṉa eṉṟē.”



eṉavum avar pāṭiyuḷḷār.[26] pāy pariyiṉarāṉa islāmiyarip pōṉṟu tamiḻakattil “nakarattār” “nāṭṭuk kōṭṭiyār” eṉṟu ciṟappuṭaṉ aḻikkappaṭukiṉṟa ceṭṭi makkaḷatu maṇaviṉiyil mutalaṅkamāka, ivuḷi mītu maṇamakaṉ ivarntu maṇamakaḷ illam ceṉṟu maṇaviṉi mēṟkoḷvatu iṉṟaḷavum iruntu varuvatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu. attuṭaṉ intiya nāṭṭiṉ piṟa mānilaṅkaḷāṉa kāṣmīr, rājastāṉ, pañcāp, uttirappiratēcam, mattiya piratēcam ākiya mānilaṅkaḷil uḷḷa makkaḷiṭamum inta paḻakkam iruntu varukiṟatu. itaṉi, aṅku “parāt” (pavaṉi) yeṉa aḻikkiṉṟaṉar. iṉṉum viññāṉattil mikamika vaḷarcciyuṟṟa cōviyat yūṉiyaṉil kūṭa kājakistāṉil maṇamakaṉ, maṇamakaḷit taṉatu kutiriyil ēṟṟikkoṇṭu ūruṅkiḷiyum uṭaṉvara, maṇaviṉip pativu aluvalakattiṟkuc celvatu iṉṟaḷavu vaḻakkamāka iruntu varukiṟatu.

ivvitam, tamiḻakattil arapik kutirikaḷuṭaṉ vantu taṅki nilitta tamiḻaka iculāmiyarāṉa rāvuttarkaḷatu uṟiviṭam, “rāvuttar” eṉṟa vikutiyuṭaṉ pala ūrkaḷ amintu uḷḷaṉa. avikaḷil cila :

1. rāvutta nallūṉar — ﻿ kaḷḷakkuṟicci vaṭṭam

2. rāvutta nallūr — ﻿ kāñcipuram vaṭṭam

3. rāvuttaṉpaṭṭi — ﻿ kuḷittali vaṭṭam

4. rāvuttaṉpaṭṭi — ﻿ tirumaṅkalam vaṭṭam

5. rāvuttaṉ vayal — ﻿ paṭṭukkōṭṭi vaṭṭam





|- | 6. || rāvuttar pāḷiyam ||﻿—﻿|| tirunelvēli vaṭṭam |- | 7. || rāvuttar cāyapu tarkā ||﻿—﻿|| aṟantāṅki vaṭṭam |- | 8. || rāvutta rāyaṉ kuppam ||﻿—﻿|| tirukkōyilūr vaṭṭam |- | 9. || rākutta rāya nallūr ||﻿—﻿|| īrōṭu vaṭṭam |}

ivi aṉittum islāmiyat tamiḻarkaḷatu toṉmiyāṉa iruppiṭaṅkaḷ eṉpatil aiyamilli. mēlum, rāvuttarukkum kutirikkum uḷḷa campantatti terivikkum vaḻakku irāmanātapuram māvaṭṭattil “kutirikkāka rāvuttar. kokkākap paṟantār” eṉpatu iṉṟum vaḻakkil uḷḷatu. āṉāl tamiḻakameṅkum uḷḷa rāvuttar vaḻiyiṉariṭam iṉṟu kutiriyum illi; kutiric cēvakamum toḻilāka illi eṉpatu yāvarum aṟinta uṇmi.

6

marakkāyar





tamiḻaka islāmiyarkaḷil iṉṉoru piriviṉar marakkalarāyar eṉṟa marikkāyarkaḷ. patiṉintām nūṟṟāṇṭil, pōrccukal nāṭṭu paraṅkikaḷatu kaṭal valimiyiṉāl arapik kaṭal vaḻi pātikkappaṭṭu koḷḷiyar vaḻiyāka māṟiyatu. arapikkuṭā nāṭukaḷil nilaviya palamaṟṟa araciyal, arapukaḷatu veḷināṭṭu vaṇikattiṟku muṟṟuppuḷḷi vittatu. avarkaḷ tamiḻakattil kuṭiyēṟi, tamiḻc camutāyattiṉ oru pakutiyāka nirantaramāka tamiḻ maṇṇil nili koḷvataṟkāṉa nirpantatti ūkkuvittatu. itaṉāl, pōrccukīsiyaruṭaṉ pōriṭṭum avarkaḷatu kīḻi nāṭṭu vaṇikattil pōṭṭiyiṭa muṭiyāta tamiḻaka islāmiyar kaṭalōra uḷnāṭṭu vāṇipattil kavaṉattic celuttiṉar. attuṭaṉ, aṇmi nāṭukaḷāṉa vaṅkāḷam ilaṅki, parmā, ākiya nāṭukaḷuṭaṉ arici, tēkkumaram, kittaṟittuṇi, āṭu māṭukaḷ, ākiyavikaḷit taṅkaḷ marakkalaṅkaḷil eṭuttuc ceṉṟuviṟṟaṉar. mēlum, avarkaḷ mēṟkoṇṭu irunta palvakippaṭṭa toḻilik kīḻkkaṇṭa paḻam pāṭal[1] vivarikkiṉṟatu.

"nīrōṭṭañ caṅkeṭuttal. nittila calāpa, nīnti nīrk kuḷittal, valiyiṉ mīṉ iḻuttal





pār nīṭṭa maṟital, mīṉ ulattal, mīṉ viṟṟal, pāṟu mutaṟpi ḷōppal, veḷiruppup paṭuttal,





kārōṭṭaṅ kāṇal, mīṉ kōṭ paṟiyōṭatti kaṇṭi kōṭṭal, vali pāṭṭu kaḻaṟa mpiyōṭṭal





cūḻ kāṭṭal, piṟartēcam pukutal .... .... ...".





﻿perumpālum, kiḻakku kaṭaṟkariyōrap paṭṭiṉaṅkaḷāṉa nākapaṭṭiṉam, atirāmpaṭaṭiṉam, pācippaṭṭiṉam, toṇṭi, tēvi paṭṭiṉam, pāmpaṉ irāmēsvaram, vētāḷi, periyapaṭṭiṉam, kīḻakkari, tūttukkuṭi. kāyalpaṭṭiṉam, ākiya ūrkaḷil uḷḷa islāmiyar inta toḻilkaḷil aṇmikkālam vari īṭupaṭṭu iruntaṉar. ivarkaḷ, tōṇi, ṭiṅki, cāmpāṉ eṉṟa vakiyāṉa marakkalaṅkaḷit taṅkaḷ toḻilukku payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. avarkaḷ marikkāyar, (marakkalarāyar) eṉavum vaḻaṅkappaṭṭaṉar. marakkalam eṉṟa poruḷit tarukiṟa "markkap” eṉṟa arapuc colliṉ tamiḻ ākkamē marikkār eṉpatu pērāciriyar mukammatu hūsiṉ nayiṉār avarkaḷatu muṭivu. tamiḻaka islāmiyariṉ oru piriviṉari, - taṅkaḷukku oppārum mikkārumiṉṟi kaṭal vaṇikattil īṭupaṭṭirunta islāmiyarik-kuṟikka payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭa tamiḻc collē' marikkāyar eṉpatu. marakkalarāyar eṉṟa nalla tamiḻiṉ tiripu tāṉ marikkār allatu marikkāyar. kutirip paṭiyiṉ taḷapatiyik kuṟikka vaḻaṅkiya rākuttarāyaṉ eṉṟa col rāvut taṉ eṉat tirintāṟ pōla marakkalaṅkaḷiṉ talivaṉ eṉṟa peyaril marakkalarāyar eṉa puṉiyappaṭṭuḷḷa piṟkālat tamiḻc col iṅṅṉam tiripu peṟṟuḷḷatu.[2] itaṉip pōṉṟu marakkalam campantappaṭṭa islāmiyarik kuṟikka “ōṭāḷi” (ōṭāvi) eṉṟa peyarum uḷḷatu, āṉāl intac col kīḻakkari, toṇṭi pōṉṟa paḻamiyāṉa kaṭaṟkari ūrkaḷil “ōṭāḷi” (ōṭāvi) eṉa vaḻakku peṟṟuḷḷatu. ōṭatti āḷpavaṉ eṉṟa poruḷil.

aṇmi nāṭāṉa ilaṅkiyil, islāmiyarkaḷi ciṅkaḷa moḻiyil marakkala miṉicu’’ eṉṟē vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉar.[3]marakkalattil vanta (vaṇika) makkaḷ eṉṉum poruḷil, tamiḻp patamāṉa marakkalam, ciṅkaḷattilum ticic collāka vaḻaṅkukiṟatu. "kaṭṭumaram" eṉṟa taṉittamiḻc col, āṅkila moḻiyil “kaṭṭamarāṉ” eṉṟāl pōla, ittakiya vaṇikattil payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭuḷḷa marakkalattiṟku oru vakiyāṉa iṟi vitikkappaṭṭatu. cekkiṟi, taṟiiṟi, pōṉṟu marakkalak kāṇam eṉpatē anta variyākum. itē col piṟkālattil marakkāṇam eṉa maruviyuḷḷatu. ceṅki māvaṭṭattil ceṉṉi paṭṭiṉattiṟku arukil uḷḷa kaṭal tuṟi oṉṟiṉukku marak kāṇam eṉṟa peyarē nilittuviṭṭatu. inta ūrip pōṉṟu, irāmanātapuram māvaṭṭattil “marikkāyar" eṉṟa peyaruṭaṉ kaṭaṟṟuṟi yoṉṟu maṇṭapattiṟku aṇmiyil "marikkāyar paṭṭiṉam" eṉa vaḻaṅki varukiṟatu. kīḻakkariyil vāṇipac celvākkil mikuntu niṉṟa hapīpu marakkāyar eṉpavar niṉivāka eḻuntatu inta ūr. maṟṟum, putukkōṭṭi māvaṭṭattil uḷḷa patiṉintāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭu kalveṭṭu oṉṟil "marakkalac cutantirama" eṉṟatoru toṭar kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.[4] rakkalaṅkaḷ amikkat takunta maraṅkaḷik kāṭukaḷil iruttu veṭṭikkoḷḷum urimitāṉ iṅṅṉam kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.

inta marikkāyaril oru piriviṉar - "taṇṭal” eṉa aḻikkappaṭṭuvantaṉar. itaṉatu nēraṭiyāṉa poruḷ paṭakuttalivaṉ eṉpati tamiḻil maṭṭum allāmal, taṇṭal (ciṅkaḷam)taṇṭelu (teluṅku) tantal (maliyāḷam)taṇṭēl (urutu) (arapi) ākiya piṟa moḻic coṟkaḷilum vaḻaṅkappaṭuvatu iṅku cintikkattakkatu. kaṭal vāṇipattiṉ piṟitoru pakutiyāṉa mīṉ vaṇikattil īṭupaṭṭuḷḷa iṉṉoru piriviṉar "cammāṭṭi" eṉṟum aḻikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. mīṉ piṭittalukkup payaṉpaṭum naṭuttara vakiyāṉa “cāmpāṉ” eṉṟa vaḷḷatti iyakkupavar eṉṟa poruḷil cāmpāṉ ōṭṭi kālappōkkil cammāṭṭi yāṉatākat terikiṟatu. irāmanātapuram kaṭaṟkarip pakutikaḷil uḷḷa intu, islāmiyar ākiya iru piriviṉarāṉa mīṉ vaṇikarkaḷum cammāṭṭi eṉṟē kuṟikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. cittūr tarṣaṇap paḷḷiyiṉ ki.pi. 1409m āṇṭu kalveṭṭilum paramakkuṭi vaṭṭam emaṉēsvaram kirā﻿mattu ki.pi. 1567m āṇṭu kalveṭṭilum "cammāṭṭi" eṉṟa col kiyāḷappaṭṭuḷḷatu.[5] etirkkariyāṉa īḻattil vaṇikarkaḷik kuṟikka "cammāṅkārar" eṉṟa vaḻakku uḷḷatu. cammāntuṟi” eṉṟa ūrum aṅku kiḻakku mākāṇattil uḷḷatu. malēciyā moḻiyilum, vaṇikarkaḷ “hammaṅkarāya" eṉṟa mūṉṟu coṟkaḷukkum vaṇikam eṉṟa poruḷil toṭarpu uḷḷatu[6] . kāraṇam inta nāṭukaḷuṭaṉ tamiḻaka islāmiyarkaḷ vaṇika toṭarpukaḷuṭaṉ maṇa uṟavukaḷum koṇṭiruntatu, itaṟku kāraṇamāka irukkalām. inta islāmiyar, īḻattil "marikkār" eṉṟa piriviṉarāka vaḻaṅkappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl, parmā, malēṣiyā nāṭukaḷil ivarkaḷ "cōḻiya muslīmkaḷ" eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. patiṉōrām, paṉṉiraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil, tamiḻakam muḻutum cōḻarkaḷatu ceṅkoṟṟak kuṭiyiṉ kīḻ iruntatāl tamiḻakattiṉ kiḻakku kaṭaṟkari muḻuvatum cōḻa maṇṭalak kari eṉṟē kuṟikkappaṭaṭatu. (intac colli piṟkāla āṅkilēyarum, cōḻa maṇṭalak kari muḻuvatiyum "kōramāṇṭal kōlaṭ” eṉappeyariṭṭu aḻittaṉar). intak kariyiṉiṉṟum anta nāṭukaḷukkuc ceṉṟu kuṭiyēṟiya muslīmkaḷ eṉṟa kāraṇattiṉāl "cōḻiya" (cōḻa nāṭṭu) eṉṟa col cērkkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. raṅkūṉilum (parmā) piṉāṅkilum (malēṣiyā)cōḻiya muslim paḷḷikaḷum, caṅkaṅkaḷum, vītikaḷum iruntu varuvatu iṅku kuṟippiṭttakkatu.

marikkāyar paṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷik kuṟippiṭum poḻutu marikkāyaril patiṉēḻāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ piṟpakutiyil, cemmāntu niṉṟa cītakkātiyiṉ pukaḻvaṭivu nam maṉattiriyil varipaṭamāka vaḷarttōṅki viḷaṅkukiṟatu. kīḻakkariyi tāyakamākak koṇṭa vaḷḷal ṣeyku aptul kātiṟu marikkāyar avarkaḷ tammi nāṭi vantavarkaḷukku, poṉṉum poruḷum, īntu tamiḻ vaḷḷalkaḷ vaḻiyilē tamiḻ marapu kāttār. eḻuvāṉiyum toṭuvāṉiyum toṭarntu ceṉṟa avaratu nāvāykaḷ, cumantu vanta celva maṉittum ītal aṟattiṟku īṭāka niṉṟaṉa. perum pulavarkaḷāṉa paṭikkācut tampirāṉum namaccivāyap pulavarum umaṟu kattāp pulavarum iyaṟṟiya pākkaḷ avaratu vaḷḷaṇmikku kāla mellām kaṭṭiyaṅkaṟi, avaratu pukaḻip pōṟṟi varukiṉṟaṉa.[7] vaḷḷal avarkaḷatu vāṇipac ciṟappi perum pulavar umaṟu kattāppiṉ "cītakkāti tirumaṇa vāḻttu’’ pākkaḷ vaḻikāṇalām. tirikālamum uṇarnta tavac celvar ñāṉi catakkattullāvi ācāṉākavum, vaṇaṅkāmuṭi vēntarāṉa vijaya rakunāta (kiḻavaṉ) cētupatiyi uṭaṉpiṟavāc cōtararākavum koṇṭirunta inta vaḷḷali, tamiḻ vaḻaṅkum aintiṇi yeṅkum, cettum koṭutta cītakkāti eṉa, ceṉṟa irunūṟu āṇṭukaḷukku mēlāka muḻaṅki varuvatē avaratu nalliyalpukaḷukku niliyāṉa cāṉṟākum.



↑ pirapanta tiraṭṭu (1982) pāṭal eṇ 351

↑ itē col aṇṭi nāṭukaḷāṉa ilaṅkiyilum, kēraḷattilum cila pakutikaḷil uḷḷa islāmiyarik kuṟikkum iṉappeyarākavum vaḻaṅkappaṭṭu varukiṉṟatu.

↑ piṟiyaṉpaṉ - kaliyum paṇpāṭum (1962) pak. 58,

↑ Pudukotti State Inscriptions No : 58

↑ A.R. 392/1914 - (paramakkuṭi)

↑ A.R. 324 / 1912 - (tirṣaṇappaḷḷi)

↑ piṟiyaṉpaṉ - kaliyum paṇpāṭum (ilaṅki)1962 - pak. 58





7

leppi





iṅṅaṉam, tamiḻc camutāyattiṉarāṉa islāmiyar nāḷaṭivil turukkar, cōṉakar, rāvuttar, marikkāyar eṉṟu piriviṉip peyarkaḷāl piṟkālaṅkaḷil, leppi, nayiṉār, tarakaḷurmutaliyār ampalam, cērvi eṉṟa peyar vikutikaḷi iṇittuk koṇṭaṉar. avarkaḷ vāḻnta antantap pakutiyil uḷḷa itara piriviṉarkaḷiṉ peyarālē ivarkaḷatu antap peyar vikutikaḷum amintaṉa. leppi kuṭikkāṭu (tirucci māvaṭṭam) nayiṉār kōvil, nayiṉār pēṭṭi. (mukavi māvaṭṭam) nayiṉārpuram (pacumpoṉ māvaṭṭam) nayiṉār akaram (nelli māvaṭṭam) eṉṟu avarkaḷatu ūrp peyarkaḷum anta makkaḷatu vikutippeyaruṭaṉ vaḻaṅkappaṭukiṟatu īṇṭu kuṟippiṭattakkaṉavākum. "leppi" eṉṟa col tamiḻakamaṉittum parantu vāḻum ellāp pakutiyilum uḷḷa islāmiyarik kuṟippiṭum potuc collāka uḷḷatu. araput tāyakattil iruntu vantu nāḷaṭivil tamiḻ muslimkaḷāka māṟiya poḻutum, avarkaḷatu taliyāya kaṭami tirumaṟiyi ōtiyum, piṟarukku ōtuvittu uṇarttum paṇiyāka iruntatu. eṉṟālum, ivarkaḷatu vāḻkki nili, necavāḷiyāka, vivacāyiyāka, kaṭal toḻilāḷiyāka iruntu vantatu. āṉāl, ivarkaḷ patiṉēḻu, patiṉeṭṭāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭuvari leppikaḷ eṉṟa potup peyarilēyē vaḻaṅkappaṭṭaṉar. yākkōpu cittaratu pāṭal oṉṟil “collavē nālvēta leppimār eṉak kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. mārkkappaṇiyil muṉintu iruppavarkaḷ eṉṟa poruḷil 1881m āṇṭu āṅkilēyaratu makkaḷ kaṇakku aṟikki, leppikaḷ paṟṟik kuṟippiṭumpoḻutu, avarkaḷ tañci, maturi irāmanātapuram uḷḷiṭṭa māvaṭṭaṅkaḷil piratāṉamāka vāḻntu varuvatākavum, mēṟkukkari muslīm, "māppiḷḷikaḷip pōla ivarkaḷum cōḻa maṇṭalakkari māppiḷḷi" yeṉa varuṇittuḷḷatu. arāpiya irattak kalappuṭaṉ kūṭiya matam māṟiya tirāviṭarkaḷum intukkaḷumāṉa ivarkaḷ cuṟu cuṟuppum, muṉṉēṟṟa maṉappāṅkum mikka viyāpārikaḷ eṉavum anta aṟikki kuṟippiṭukiṟatu. ivarkaḷil perumpāṉmiyiṉar vaṇikattilum, koṭikkāl vivacāyattilum, cilar, necavu, caṅku, muttukkuḷittal, camayappaṇi ākiya toḻilkaḷilum īṭupaṭṭuḷḷatākavum aṟivikkiṟatu[1]

inta aracu aṟikkiyi aṭuttu veḷi vantuḷḷa maturi kejaṭṭīr, "leppikaḷ nērttiyāṉa, uṟutiyāṉa , ceyaltiṟam mikka makkaḷ eṉaṟum, entac cūḻniliyiyum camāḷittu naṭantu koḷḷakkūṭiyavarkaḷ’’ eṉṟum pukaḻuri vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷatu.[2] ivarkaḷil perumpāṉmiyiṉar vaṇikattilum, ciṟitaḷaviṉar kiviṉik kalikaḷ, kaṭal toḻil, pōṉṟa tuṟikaḷil īṭupaṭṭiruppatākak kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. toṇṭi maṇṭalattil uḷḷa veppikaḷ veṟṟilik koṭikkāl vivacāyikaḷākavum, tōl viyāpārikaḷākavum, ciṟu toḻil vaṇikarkaḷāvum avarkaḷatu peṇ pālar, pāy necavu toḻilil tiṟami uḷḷavarkaḷāka iruppatāka teṉ āṟkāṭu māvaṭṭam kejaṭṭīr[3] kuṟippiṭukiṟatu. vaṭa āṟkāṭu māvaṭṭa leppikaḷ, vacati uḷḷavarkaḷākavum tirumaṟippaṭi vāḻviyalil vāḻvāṅku vāḻpavarkaḷākavum ﻿vaṭa āṟkāṭu maṉuvalil āciriyar sṭūvarṭ kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. irāmanātapuram māvaṭṭa kiḻakkup pakutiyil leppikaḷ necavilum vivacāyattilum mikuntu iruppatāka irāmanātapuram māṉuvalil variyappaṭṭuḷḷatu.[4] cittārkōṭṭi, ekkakuṭi, paṉikkuḷam, pēriyūr, kamuti, apirāmam ākiya ūrkaḷil avarkaḷ muṉṉar necavil īṭupaṭṭiruntatik kuṟikkum "pāvōṭi"kaḷ iṉṟum uḷḷaṉa. mēlum aruppuk kōṭṭi, kaṭiyanallūr, puḷiyaṅkuṭi muslimkaḷ necavil mikutiyāka toṭarntu īṭupaṭṭiruppatu inta kūṟṟiṉit valiyuṟuttuvatāka uḷḷatu.

maṟṟum "nayiṉār" "mutaliyār" vikutikaḷiyuṭiya islāmiyap peyarkaḷ tirunelvēli māvaṭṭattilum "tarakaṉār" eṉṟa col kumari māvaṭṭattilum “ampalam”, "cērvi" vikutikaḷiyuṭiya islāmiyap peyarkaḷ, irāmanātapuram, pacumpoṉ, maturi māvaṭṭaṅkaḷilum iṉṟaḷavum vaḻakkil uḷḷaṉa. pacumpoṉ māvaṭṭattil tiruppattūr, iḷiyāṅkuṭi, irāmanātapuram māvaṭṭattil rāja ciṅkamaṅkalam, irāmanātapuram, kīḻakkari, periyapaṭṭiṉam, maṇṭapam, toṇṭi ākiya ūrkaḷilum maturi māvaṭṭattil, tiṇṭukkal, paḻani, vettilikkuṇṭu ākiya ūrkaḷilum "ampalam" eṉṟa col islāmiyap peyarkaḷuṭaṉ iṇintu olikkiṉṟaṉa. maṟṟum maturi, mēlūr pakutikaḷil "nāṭṭāṇmi" eṉṟa peyar vaḻakkil uḷḷatu. irāmanātapuram cētu maṉṉariṉ cēviyil ciṟantu paṇiyāṟṟiya apirāmam nūrmukammatu eṉpavarukku cētupati maṉṉar ciṟappukaḷ ceytu kuravittatuṭaṉ "vijayaṉ ampalam" eṉṟa virutup peyariyum vaḻaṅkiṉār. inta nikaḻcci naṭipeṟṟu irunūṟu āṇṭukaḷākiṉṟaṉa. avaratu vaḻi vanta vaḷḷal oruvari pāṭṭuṭittalivarākak koṇṭu maturi tamiḻc caṅkap pulavarāṉa cōtukuṭi aptulkātar rāvuttar eṉpavar "akapporuḷ kōvi" oṉṟippāṭi uḷḷār.[5] maṟṟum. paramakkuṭi, mutukuḷattūr vaṭṭāraṅkaḷil islāmiyar cilar taṅkaḷ peyaruṭaṉ “cērvi” eṉṟa colliyum iṇittup payaṉpaṭuttukiṉṟaṉar. mottattil “ampalam” “cērvi” eṉṟa iru coṟkaḷum antanta ūrkaḷil camūkat talivar eṉṟa poruḷil tāṉ pirayōkikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. maturi māvaṭṭattil ēṉiya intu camūkat talivarip pōṉṟu islāmiyat camūkat talivarum "nāṭṭāṇmi" eṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. intac coṟkaḷiṉ vaḻakku paṟṟi caṟṟu tīviramākac cintittāl, intat tamiḻ maṇṇil islām evvaḷavu āḻamāka valuvāka, vēr parantuḷḷatu eṉpatu viḷaṅkum. tamiḻnāṭṭil, mutaṉmutalil irāmanātapuram, nelli, maturi māvaṭṭaṅkaḷil arāpiyaratu pūrva kuṭiyēṟṟaṅkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭatāl, anta islāmiyarkaḷ, aṅkuḷḷa makkaḷuṭaṉ nāḷaṭivil nerukkamāṉa toṭarpum tōḻamiyum, uṟavum koṇṭu, tamiḻc camūka amippil uṟutiyāka ūṭuruvi, iṇintu iṉṟaḷavum niṟpatuvē ittakiya arapu tamiḻp peyarkaḷ iṇippiṟku kāraṇam eṉpatum puriyum kaṭanta cila nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil, araciyal, poruḷātārac cūḻnili kāraṇamāka pōrccukkīciyar, ṭakcukkārar āṅkilēyar, pireñcukkārar, pōṉṟa piṟa nāṭṭavar tamiḻnāṭṭiṟku vantuḷḷaṉar. tamiḻc camutāyattil kalantum irukkiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl, arapunāṭṭu islāmiyarkaḷip pōṉṟu, inta maṇṇiṉ māṭciyi, pārampariya viḻutukaḷi eṉṟeṉṟum valuvākap paṟṟi niṟkum peṟṟi, paṇpāṭu, uḷḷappāṅku avarkaḷukku amiyavilli eṉpatu varalāṟu uṇarttum uvamiyākum.

↑ Frana's W. - Maduri Gazetteer (1921)

↑ Rajaram Roa - S. Manuel of Ramnad Samasthanam (1898)p. 49
